### [1] [From Spark to Fire: Modeling and Mitigating Error Cascades in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04474)
*Yizhe Xie,Congcong Zhu,Xinyue Zhang,Tianqing Zhu,Dayong Ye,Minfeng Qi,Huajie Chen,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出针对LLM多智能体系统的传播动力学模型，识别三类脆弱性，并开发基于谱系图的治理层插件来抑制错误放大


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在复杂协作场景中，微小不准确会通过迭代固化为系统级错误共识，现有保护方法依赖单智能体验证或需修改协作架构，可能削弱有效信息流且不符合真实任务的自然协作过程

Method: 提出针对LLM-MAS的传播动力学模型，将协作抽象为有向依赖图并提供早期风险准则；识别三类脆弱性；开发基于谱系图的治理层插件，作为消息层插件实现，不改变协作架构

Result: 在六个主流框架上的实验识别出三类脆弱性：级联放大、拓扑敏感性和共识惯性；攻击实验显示注入单个原子错误种子即可导致广泛失败；治理层将防御成功率从基线0.32提升至超过0.89，显著缓解微小错误的级联传播

Conclusion: LLM-MAS存在系统性错误放大风险，提出的传播动力学模型能有效识别脆弱性，基于谱系图的治理层插件可在不改变协作架构的情况下抑制内生和外生错误放大，显著提升系统鲁棒性

Abstract: Large Language Model-based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly applied to complex collaborative scenarios. However, their collaborative mechanisms may cause minor inaccuracies to gradually solidify into system-level false consensus through iteration. Such risks are difficult to trace since errors can propagate and amplify through message dependencies. Existing protections often rely on single-agent validation or require modifications to the collaboration architecture, which can weaken effective information flow and may not align with natural collaboration processes in real tasks. To address this, we propose a propagation dynamics model tailored for LLM-MAS that abstracts collaboration as a directed dependency graph and provides an early-stage risk criterion to characterize amplification risk. Through experiments on six mainstream frameworks, we identify three vulnerability classes: cascade amplification, topological sensitivity, and consensus inertia. We further instantiate an attack where injecting just a single atomic error seed leads to widespread failure. In response, we introduce a genealogy-graph-based governance layer, implemented as a message-layer plugin, that suppresses both endogenous and exogenous error amplification without altering the collaboration architecture. Experiments show that this approach raises the defense success rate from a baseline of 0.32 to over 0.89 and significantly mitigates the cascading spread of minor errors.

</details>


### [2] [SCoUT: Scalable Communication via Utility-Guided Temporal Grouping in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04833)
*Manav Vora,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Hiroyasu Tsukamoto,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: SCoUT是一种可扩展的多智能体强化学习方法，通过时间分组和效用引导的通信机制，解决了部分可观测环境中何时通信、与谁通信的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的多智能体强化学习中，通信可以改善协调，但学习何时通信、与谁通信面临两个主要挑战：需要从大量可能的发送者-接收者对中选择，以及单个消息对未来奖励的影响难以隔离。

Method: SCoUT通过时间分组和智能体抽象解决通信问题：1) 每K个环境步骤（宏步骤）通过Gumbel-Softmax重新采样软智能体分组；2) 使用相同的分组分配，组感知评论家为每个智能体组预测价值并映射到每个智能体的基线；3) 每个智能体训练三头策略：环境动作、发送决策和接收者选择；4) 通过分析移除每个发送者对接收者聚合消息的贡献，推导反事实通信优势，实现精确的信用分配。

Result: 该方法在训练时保持集中化，执行时丢弃所有集中化训练组件，仅运行每个智能体的策略，保持去中心化执行。项目网站、视频和代码已公开。

Conclusion: SCoUT通过时间分组和效用引导的通信机制，有效解决了多智能体强化学习中通信的信用分配问题，实现了可扩展的通信策略学习，同时保持了去中心化执行的优势。

Abstract: Communication can improve coordination in partially observed multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), but learning \emph{when} and \emph{who} to communicate with requires choosing among many possible sender-recipient pairs, and the effect of any single message on future reward is hard to isolate. We introduce \textbf{SCoUT} (\textbf{S}calable \textbf{Co}mmunication via \textbf{U}tility-guided \textbf{T}emporal grouping), which addresses both these challenges via temporal and agent abstraction within traditional MARL. During training, SCoUT resamples \textit{soft} agent groups every \(K\) environment steps (macro-steps) via Gumbel-Softmax; these groups are latent clusters that induce an affinity used as a differentiable prior over recipients. Using the same assignments, a group-aware critic predicts values for each agent group and maps them to per-agent baselines through the same soft assignments, reducing critic complexity and variance. Each agent is trained with a three-headed policy: environment action, send decision, and recipient selection. To obtain precise communication learning signals, we derive counterfactual communication advantages by analytically removing each sender's contribution from the recipient's aggregated messages. This counterfactual computation enables precise credit assignment for both send and recipient-selection decisions. At execution time, all centralized training components are discarded and only the per-agent policy is run, preserving decentralized execution. Project website, videos and code: \hyperlink{https://scout-comm.github.io/}{https://scout-comm.github.io/}

</details>

### [3] [How Professional Visual Artists are Negotiating Generative AI in the Workplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04537)
*Harry H. Jiang,Jordan Taylor,William Agnew*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对378名专业视觉艺术家的调查显示，大多数艺术家强烈反对使用生成式AI，采用多种拒绝策略，面临来自客户、老板和同行的压力，并报告生成式AI对其工作场所产生负面影响的普遍现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI在流行媒体和HCI研究中受到艺术家批评，但对其如何影响专业艺术家工作场所和职业的研究仍然不足。本研究旨在深入了解生成式AI对专业视觉艺术家职业和工作场所的实际影响。

Method: 对378名经过验证的专业视觉艺术家进行问卷调查，研究生成式AI（文本或视觉）如何影响他们的职业和工作场所。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 大多数视觉艺术家强烈反对使用生成式AI，并通过多种拒绝策略来应对工作场所中的AI应用；(2) 艺术家环境中存在多种因素影响他们对生成式AI的使用，包括来自客户、老板和同行的压力；(3) 视觉艺术家普遍报告生成式AI对其工作场所产生负面影响，导致压力增加和工作机会减少。

Conclusion: 基于这些发现，作者鼓励HCI研究人员更深入地考虑艺术家在工作场所中不希望使用生成式AI的意愿，并建议未来研究应更多关注艺术家对AI技术的抵制和拒绝策略。

Abstract: Generative AI has been heavily critiqued by artists in both popular media and HCI scholarship. However, more work is needed to understand the impacts of generative AI on professional artists' workplaces and careers. In this paper, we conduct a survey of \textit{378 verified professional visual artists} about how generative AI has impacted their careers and workplaces. We find (1) most visual artists are strongly opposed to using generative AI (text or visual) and negotiate their inclusion in the workplace through a variety of \textit{refusal} strategies (2) there exist a range of factors in artists environments shaping their use of generative AI, including pressure from clients, bosses, and peers and (3) visual artists report overwhelmingly negative impacts of generative AI on their workplaces, leading to added stress and reduced job opportunities. In light of these findings, we encourage HCI researchers to contend more deeply with artists' desires not to use generative AI in the workplace.

</details>


### [4] [Beyond the Interface: Redefining UX for Society-in-the-Loop AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04552)
*Nahal Mafi,Sahar Maleki,Babak Rahimi Ardabili,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出AI人机协同系统中用户体验需超越前端可用性，涵盖后端性能、组织流程和决策结构，并建立了包含准确性、操作延迟、适应时间和信任度的四维度社会技术评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统用户体验框架针对确定性系统设计，无法捕捉AI人机协同系统中概率性输出和人类参与带来的社会技术动态变化。AI系统在决策关键环境中日益普及，需要新的评估方法。

Method: 采用混合方法：1）对269个利益相关者见解进行归纳性社会建构分析；2）部署操作性的HITL视频异常检测系统进行实证研究。

Result: 研究发现利益相关者通过风险、治理和组织能力等多维度主题体验AI。实验结果显示检测行为和警报路由直接影响人类监督和工作负载。基于此建立了包含准确性（FPR/FNR）、操作延迟（响应时间）、适应时间（部署负担）和信任度（验证自动化量表）的四维度社会技术评估框架。

Conclusion: 用户体验应重新定义为跨越基础设施和治理的多层结构，为评估嵌入复杂现实生态系统中的AI系统提供严谨基础。新框架将UX从单纯的前端可用性扩展到包含后端性能和组织流程的全面评估体系。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems increasingly operate in decision-critical environments where probabilistic outputs and Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) interactions reshape user engagement. Traditional user experience (UX) frameworks, designed for deterministic systems, fail to capture these evolving sociotechnical dynamics. This paper argues that in AI-enabled HITL systems, UX must transcend frontend usability to encompass backend performance, organizational workflows, and decision making structures.
  We employ a mixed-methods approach, combining an inductive social construction analysis of 269 stakeholder insights with the deployment of an operational HITL video anomaly detection system. Our findings reveal that stakeholders experience AI through multifaceted themes: risk, governance, and organizational capacity. Experimental results further demonstrate how detection behavior and alert routing directly calibrate human oversight and workload. Grounded in these results, we formalize a new evaluative framework centered on four sociotechnical metrics: Accuracy (FPR/FNR), Operational Latency (response time), Adaptation Time (deployment burden), and Trust (validated automation scales). This framework redefines UX as a multi-layered construct spanning infrastructure and governance, providing a rigorous foundation for evaluating AI systems embedded within complex real-world ecosystems.

</details>


### [5] [Beyond Anthropomorphism: a Spectrum of Interface Metaphors for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04613)
*Jianna So,Connie Cheng,Sonia Krishna Murthy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出将拟人化作为设计变量，开发从"反拟人化"到"超拟人化"的隐喻谱系，以打破LLM界面的默认拟人化倾向，促进批判性参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM界面过度强化拟人化隐喻，用户常将其视为真人，导致错觉和伤害。拟人化界面突出了LLM与人类的相似性，但掩盖了关键差异，缺乏保障措施。

Method: 将拟人化重新定位为设计变量，构建从透明度驱动的"反拟人化"到怪异的"超拟人化"的隐喻谱系。这些隐喻为界面隐喻引入物质性，揭示LLM作为由人类劳动、基础设施和数据塑造的社会技术系统。

Result: 提出一个理论框架，展示界面隐喻如何塑造和打破默认隐喻。该谱系将界面设计从优化可用性转向鼓励批判性参与，暴露LLM的社会技术本质。

Conclusion: 需要重新思考LLM界面设计，将拟人化作为可控变量而非默认设置，通过极端隐喻谱系促进用户对AI系统的批判性理解，避免有害的拟人化错觉。

Abstract: Anthropomorphizing conversational technology is a natural human tendency. Today, the anthropomorphic metaphor is overly reinforced across intelligent tools. Large Language Models (LLMs) are particularly anthropomorphized through interface design. While metaphors are inherently partial, anthropomorphic interfaces highlight similarities between LLMs and humans, but mask crucial differences. As a result, the metaphor is often taken literally; users treat LLMs as if they are truly human. With few safeguards in place, this extreme anthropomorphism drives users to delusion and harm. Users also experience dissonance between the ethics of using LLMs, their growing ubiquity, and limited interface alternatives. We propose repositioning anthropomorphism as a design variable, developing opposing extremes as a theoretical framework for how interface metaphors shape and can disrupt the default metaphor. We introduce a spectrum of metaphors from transparency-driven ''anti-anthropomorphism'' to uncanny ''hyper-anthropomorphism''. These metaphors introduce materiality to interface metaphors, exposing LLMs as sociotechnical systems shaped by human labor, infrastructure, and data. This spectrum shifts interface design away from optimizing usability and toward encouraging critical engagement.

</details>


### [6] [Gamified Informed Decision-Making for Performance-Aware Design by Non-Experts: An Exoskeleton Design Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04643)
*Arman Khalilbeigi Khameneh,Armin Mostafavi,Alicia Nahmad Vazquez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种游戏化的决策支持框架，将游戏引擎与实时性能反馈相结合，帮助非专业设计师探索复杂的性能驱动设计空间，并在外骨骼幕墙改造项目中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策支持系统（DSS）在帮助非专业设计师探索复杂的性能驱动设计空间方面至关重要。当前需要一种方法让非专家能够理解并参与涉及结构、环境、制造等多重性能标准的复杂设计决策过程。

Method: 开发了一个游戏化的决策制定框架，集成游戏引擎提供实时性能反馈。性能标准包括结构行为、环境参数、制造、材料和成本考量。该框架在建筑学生和非专业设计师中进行测试，应用于现有建筑的外骨骼幕墙改造设计项目。

Result: 24名参与者能够迭代修改幕墙几何形状，同时接收三个关键标准的实时反馈：1）结构行为（挠度、质量、应力/强度比）；2）环境参数（太阳得热、供暖/制冷能耗）；3）制造考虑（制造和材料成本、机器人加工、材料设置）。游戏化反馈机制显著增强了用户理解和知情决策能力。

Conclusion: 游戏化环境通过知情决策和性能标准的定制化协商，使利益相关者能够参与设计过程。精心策划的设计空间和结构化指导相比开放式生成工具提高了效率，为入住前评估做出了贡献。

Abstract: Decision Support Systems (DSS) play a crucial role in enabling non-expert designers to explore complex, performance-driven design spaces. This paper presents a gamified decision-making framework that integrates game engines with real-time performance feedback. Performance criteria include structural behavior, environmental parameters, fabrication, material, and cost considerations. The developed design framework was tested with architecture students and non-expert designers on the design of an exoskeleton facade to retrofit an existing building. Participants (N=24) were able to iteratively modify façade geometries while receiving real-time feedback across the three key criteria: 1) structural behavior, including deflection, mass, and stress/strength ratio; 2) environmental parameters, such as solar gain and heating/cooling energy demands; and 3) fabrication considerations, including fabrication and material costs, robotic machining, and material setup. The evaluation of participant interactions reveals that gamified feedback mechanisms significantly enhance user comprehension and informed decision-making across the criteria. Further, participants' understanding of structural, material, and fabrication performance in relation to the iterative design task suggests that curated design spaces and structured guidance improve efficiency compared to open-ended generative tools. This research contributes to pre-occupancy evaluations, demonstrating how gamified environments enable stakeholder participation in the design process through informed decisionmaking and customized negotiation of performance criteria. .

</details>


### [7] [VizCrit: Exploring Strategies for Displaying Computational Feedback in a Visual Design Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04754)
*Mingyi Li,Mengyi Chen,Sarah Luo,Yining Cao,Haijun Xia,Maitraye Das,Steven P. Dow,Jane L. E*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VizCrit系统通过算法问题检测和视觉标注生成，为设计新手提供可操作性反馈。研究发现解决方案导向的反馈比教科书式反馈能减少更多设计问题并提升自我感知创造力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉设计教学中，教师常提供混合标注与文本的多模态反馈。现有理论强调可操作性反馈的重要性，但缺乏对反馈可操作性如何影响新手设计过程、创造力感知、设计原则学习和整体成果的系统研究。

Method: 开发VizCrit系统，通过算法问题检测和视觉标注生成支持可操作性反馈谱系。进行三组间受试研究(N=36)，新手在三种条件下修订设计：教科书式反馈、意识导向反馈、解决方案导向反馈。

Result: 解决方案导向反馈相比教科书式反馈，能显著减少设计问题并提高自我感知创造力。专家对创造力的评分无显著差异。不同反馈类型对设计原则学习的影响存在差异。

Conclusion: AI创意支持工具应校准反馈可操作性，帮助新手在生产力与学习成长之间取得平衡。解决方案导向反馈能提升问题解决效率，但需考虑其对设计意识发展的长期影响。

Abstract: Visual design instructors often provide multi-modal feedback, mixing annotations with text. Prior theory emphasizes the importance of actionable feedback, where "actionability" lies on a spectrum--from surfacing relevant design concepts to suggesting concrete fixes. How might creativity tools implement annotations that support such feedback, and how does the actionability of feedback impact novices' process-related behaviors, perceptions of creativity, learning of design principles, and overall outcomes? We introduce VizCrit, a system for providing computational feedback that supports the actionability spectrum, realized through algorithmic issue detection and visual annotation generation. In a between-subjects study (N=36), novices revised a design under one of three conditions: textbook-based, awareness-centered, or solution-centered feedback. We found that solution-centered feedback led to fewer design issues and higher self-perceived creativity compared with textbook-based feedback, although expert ratings on creativity showed no significant differences. We discuss the implications for AI in Creativity Support Tools, including the potential of calibrating feedback actionability to help novices balance productivity with learning, growth, and developing design awareness.

</details>


### [8] [Can LLMs Synthesize Court-Ready Statistical Evidence? Evaluating AI-Assisted Sentencing Bias Analysis for California Racial Justice Act Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04804)
*Aparna Komarla*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Redo.io是一个开源平台，利用LLM分析加州监狱记录，生成法庭可用的种族偏见统计证据，以支持根据《种族正义法案》提出的重审动议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管加州通过了《种族正义法案》(2020)，允许被告基于种族差异的统计证据挑战定罪，但政策实施滞后于立法意图，导致数百个重审机会未被识别，形成了"第二次机会缺口"。

Method: 开发Redo.io开源平台，处理通过《加州公共记录法案》获取的95,000份监狱记录；采用LLM驱动的解释层，将优势比、相对风险、卡方检验等统计方法的结果合成为连贯的叙述，并包含置信区间、样本量和数据限制等上下文信息。

Result: 平台能够生成法庭就绪的种族偏见统计证据，用于初步证据和发现动议；使用LLM-as-a-Judge框架评估显示，AI可以作为强大的描述性助手，在分析流程中实时生成证据。

Conclusion: AI可以作为强大的描述性助手，在分析流程中实时生成种族偏见证据，但需要以符合伦理的方式整合到分析流程中，以解决加州重审系统中的"第二次机会缺口"。

Abstract: Resentencing in California remains a complex legal challenge despite legislative reforms like the Racial Justice Act (2020), which allows defendants to challenge convictions based on statistical evidence of racial disparities in sentencing and charging. Policy implementation lags behind legislative intent, creating a 'second-chance gap' where hundreds of resentencing opportunities remain unidentified. We present Redo.io, an open-source platform that processes 95,000 prison records acquired under the California Public Records Act (CPRA) and generates court-ready statistical evidence of racial bias in sentencing for prima facie and discovery motions. We explore the design of an LLM-powered interpretive layer that synthesizes results from statistical methods like Odds Ratio, Relative Risk, and Chi-Square Tests into cohesive narratives contextualized with confidence intervals, sample sizes, and data limitations. Our evaluations comparing LLM performance to statisticians using the LLM-as-a-Judge framework suggest that AI can serve as a powerful descriptive assistant for real-time evidence generation when ethically incorporated in the analysis pipeline.

</details>


### [9] [SparkTales: Facilitating Cross-Language Collaborative Storytelling through Coordinator-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04806)
*Wenxin Zhao,Peng Zhang,Hansu Gu,Haoxuan Zhou,Xiaojie Huo,Lin Wang,Wen Zheng,Tun Lu,Ning Gu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SparkTales是一个智能支持系统，旨在减轻跨语言协作故事讲述中协调者的认知和组织负担，同时提升儿童的参与度和互动质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言协作故事讲述对儿童语言学习和文化发展至关重要，但实践中儿童参与度浅，协调者面临沉重的认知和组织负担，需要协调语言支持、维持儿童参与度并处理文化差异。

Method: 通过形成性研究识别协调者的需求和痛点，设计SparkTales智能支持系统，利用参与儿童的个体和共同特征，为协调者提供故事框架、多样化问题和理解导向的材料。

Result: SparkTales显著提高了协调者的指导效率和质量，同时改善了儿童的参与度，为未来支持跨语言协作的智能系统设计提供了宝贵见解。

Conclusion: SparkTales成功解决了跨语言协作故事讲述中的关键挑战，证明了智能系统在减轻协调者负担和提升儿童参与度方面的有效性，为相关领域系统设计提供了参考。

Abstract: Cross-language collaborative storytelling plays a vital role in children's language learning and cultural development, fostering both expressive ability and intercultural awareness. Yet, in practice, children's participation is often shallow, and facilitating such sessions places heavy cognitive and organizational burdens on coordinators, who must coordinate language support, maintain children's engagement, and navigate cultural differences. To address these challenges, we conducted a formative study with coordinators to identify their needs and pain points, which guided the design of SparkTales, an intelligent support system for cross-language collaborative storytelling. SparkTales leverages both individual and common characteristics of participating children to provide coordinators with story frameworks, diverse questions, and comprehension-oriented materials, aiming to reduce coordinators' workload while enhancing children's interactive engagement. Evaluation results show that SparkTales not only significantly increases coordinators' efficiency and quality of guidance but also improves children's participation, providing valuable insights for the design of future intelligent systems supporting cross-language collaboration.

</details>


### [10] [Roomify: Spatially-Grounded Style Transformation for Immersive Virtual Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04917)
*Xueyang Wang,Qinxuan Cen,Weitao Bi,Yunxiang Ma,Xin Yi,Robert Xiao,Xinyi Fu,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Roomify是一个空间锚定的虚拟环境生成系统，通过将物理房间转化为风格化虚拟空间，在保持空间结构和功能语义的同时实现沉浸式体验


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VR系统面临沉浸感与空间感知的权衡：完全虚拟化牺牲空间意识，而穿透式方案破坏临场感。需要一种既能保持物理空间结构又能实现沉浸式风格转换的解决方案

Method: 采用空间锚定转换方法，将物理空间视为"空间容器"，保持家具的关键功能和几何属性，同时实现风格化改变。结合现场3D场景理解、AI驱动的空间推理和风格感知生成，通过跨现实创作工具提供MR编辑和VR预览工作流

Result: 用户研究显示：18名VR用户的临场感比穿透式方案提升63%，比完全虚拟基线提升26%，同时保持空间意识。8名设计专业人员评估场景质量5.95/7分，创意支持6.08/7分，证实系统在多样化环境中的专业工作流价值

Conclusion: Roomify通过空间锚定转换成功解决了VR中沉浸感与空间感知的权衡问题，为个性化虚拟环境生成提供了有效解决方案，在保持物理空间功能结构的同时实现创造性表达

Abstract: We present Roomify, a spatially-grounded transformation system that generates themed virtual environments anchored to users' physical rooms while maintaining spatial structure and functional semantics. Current VR approaches face a fundamental trade-off: full immersion sacrifices spatial awareness, while passthrough solutions break presence. Roomify addresses this through spatially-grounded transformation - treating physical spaces as "spatial containers" that preserve key functional and geometric properties of furniture while enabling radical stylistic changes. Our pipeline combines in-situ 3D scene understanding, AI-driven spatial reasoning, and style-aware generation to create personalized virtual environments grounded in physical reality. We introduce a cross-reality authoring tool enabling fine-grained user control through MR editing and VR preview workflows. Two user studies validate our approach: one with 18 VR users demonstrates a 63% improvement in presence over passthrough and 26% over fully virtual baselines while maintaining spatial awareness; another with 8 design professionals confirms the system's creative expressiveness (scene quality: 5.95/7; creativity support: 6.08/7) and professional workflow value across diverse environments.

</details>


### [11] [Mind the Gap: Mapping Wearer-Bystander Privacy Tensions and Context-Adaptive Pathways for Camera Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04930)
*Xueyang Wang,Kewen Peng,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过调查和访谈系统评估了相机眼镜的隐私机制，发现旁观者与佩戴者之间存在期望差距，当前隐私增强技术存在四大权衡，提出基于上下文的自适应保护路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机眼镜在佩戴者记录功能和旁观者隐私保护之间产生根本性隐私张力，需要系统评估隐私机制以解决这一多利益相关者问题。

Method: 采用系统化的多利益相关者评估方法：1) 通过调查(N=525)量化期望-意愿差距；2) 通过配对访谈(N=20)深入分析；3) 评估12种隐私增强技术；4) 识别隐私机制中的基本权衡。

Result: 发现旁观者始终要求比佩戴者愿意提供的更强的信息透明度和保护措施，差距在敏感情境下加剧(65-90%旁观者会采取防御行动)；识别出当前隐私增强技术的四大权衡：可见性vs干扰、赋权vs负担、保护vs自主性、问责vs暴露。

Conclusion: 隐私差距反映了结构性不兼容而非善意不足，上下文是隐私可接受性的主要决定因素；提出上下文自适应路径：公共场所最小摩擦可见性、半公共环境结构化协商、敏感情境自动保护，为评估隐私机制和上下文感知设计提供诊断框架。

Abstract: Camera glasses create fundamental privacy tensions between wearers seeking recording functionality and bystanders concerned about unauthorized surveillance. We present a systematic multi-stakeholder evaluation of privacy mechanisms through surveys (N=525) and paired interviews (N=20) in China. Study 1 quantifies expectation-willingness gaps: bystanders consistently demand stronger information transparency and protective measures than wearers will provide, with disparities intensifying in sensitive contexts where 65-90% of bystanders would take defensive action. Study 2 evaluates twelve privacy-enhancing technologies, revealing four fundamental trade-offs that undermine current approaches: visibility versus disruption, empowerment versus burden, protection versus agency, and accountability versus exposure. These gaps reflect structural incompatibilities rather than inadequate goodwill, with context emerging as the primary determinant of privacy acceptability. We propose context-adaptive pathways that dynamically adjust protection strategies: minimal-friction visibility in public spaces, structured negotiation in semi-public environments, and automatic protection in sensitive contexts. Our findings contribute a diagnostic framework for evaluating privacy mechanisms and implications for context-aware design in ubiquitous sensing.

</details>


### [12] [Beyond Advocacy: A Design Space for Replication-Related Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04959)
*Yiheng Liang,Kim Marriott,Helen C. Purchase*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个用于复制研究设计的四维度三层次设计空间框架，支持复制研究的决策制定、分类、比较和分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复制研究在可视化、HCI及所有科学领域都很重要，但在进行复制时需要决定哪些方面保持不变、哪些需要改变，缺乏系统化的设计决策支持框架。

Method: 将复制实验设计视为成对比较问题，提出一个包含四个实践维度的设计空间框架，每个维度由三个比较层次定义，支持回顾性特征描述和前瞻性规划。

Result: 开发了一个多维设计空间框架，提供了工作示例，并将该框架与其他描述复制研究范围的尝试联系起来。

Conclusion: 该框架为复制研究的设计决策提供了系统化的支持工具，有助于识别、分类、比较和分析复制研究中的设计选择。

Abstract: The importance of replication is often discussed and advocated -- not only in the domains of visualization and HCI, but in all scientific areas. When replicating a study, design decisions need to be made with regards which aspects of the original study will remain the same and which will be altered. We present a supporting multi-dimensional design space framework within which such decisions can be identified, categorized, compared and analyzed. The framework treats replication experimental design as a pairwise comparison problem, and represents the design by four practical dimensions defined by three comparison levels. The design space is therefore a framework that can be used for both retrospective characterization and prospective planning. We provide worked examples, and relate our framework to other attempts at describing the scope of replication studies.

</details>


### [13] [Auto-Generating Personas from User Reviews in VR App Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04985)
*Yi Wang,Kexin Cheng,Xiao Liu,Chetan Arora,John Grundy,Thuong Hoang,Henry Been-Lirn Duh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了一个VR课程中的自动生成角色系统，用于促进无障碍需求讨论，帮助学生更高效地建立同理心


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色在软件设计中是讨论无障碍需求的有价值工具，但在VR项目中用于无障碍需求获取的应用有限且面临挑战，需要填补这一空白

Method: 在VR课程中开发了自动生成角色系统，使用这些角色来促进无障碍需求讨论，并指导VR设计和开发

Result: 自动生成角色系统使学生能够更高效地建立同理心，成功激发了潜在的无障碍需求

Conclusion: 该研究展示了在VR课程环境中使用自动生成角色作为获取潜在无障碍需求手段的有效性

Abstract: Personas are a valuable tool for discussing accessibility requirements in software design and development practices. However, the use of personas for accessibility-focused requirements elicitation in VR projects remains limited and is accompanied by several challenges. To fill this gap, we developed an auto-generated persona system in a VR course, where the personas were used to facilitate discussions on accessibility requirements and to guide VR design and development. Our findings indicate that the auto-generated persona system enabled students to develop empathy more efficiently. This study demonstrates the use of automatically generated personas in VR course settings as a means of eliciting latent accessibility requirements.

</details>


### [14] [Haptics in Cognition: Disruptor or Enabler of Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05019)
*Bibeg Limbu,Irene-Angelica Chounta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索性试点研究调查触觉感知（触觉敏感性和动觉强度）对手写学习中信息保持的影响，发现增加书写压力可能轻微损害即时回忆，而单独使用手套无明确效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索触觉感知（包括触觉敏感性和动觉强度）如何影响学习过程，特别是通过手写进行的信息保持，以理解具身交互与认知结果之间的复杂关系。

Method: 采用2x2因子设计的随机分组实验，20名参与者被分配到四个实验组，操纵触觉（通过使用手套）和运动（通过增加书写压力）。测量信息保持（即时回忆测试）、心理努力（次要任务反应时）和感知工作量（NASA-TLX）作为中介变量，使用贝叶斯二项回归和中介分析。

Result: 贝叶斯二项回归显示中等证据表明增加书写压力对回忆有负面影响（85-88%概率），而单独使用手套无明确效果。贝叶斯中介分析未发现心理努力或感知工作量中介这些效应的强有力证据，所有95%可信区间包含零，表明不确定性较大。

Conclusion: 增加动觉需求可能轻微损害即时回忆，且独立于感知工作量或心理努力；单独操纵触觉不影响信息保持。研究有助于理解具身交互与认知结果之间的微妙关系，对设计基于传感器的多模态学习环境有启示。

Abstract: This exploratory pilot study investigates the impact of haptic perception --specifically tactile sensitivity (touch) and kinaesthetic intensity (movement)-- on learning, operationalized as information retention (immediate recall) through handwriting. Participants (N=20) were randomly assigned to one of four experimental groups in a 2x2 factorial design, manipulating touch (via glove use) and movement (via increased writing pressure). Information retention was measured using an immediate recall test, while mental effort (reaction time in a secondary task) and perceived workload (NASA-TLX) were examined as mediating variables. Bayesian binomial regression revealed moderate evidence that increased writing pressure negatively influenced recall (85-88% probability of negative effect), whereas glove use alone demonstrated no clear effect. Bayesian mediation analysis found no strong evidence that mental effort or perceived workload mediated these effects, as all 95% credible intervals included zero, indicating substantial uncertainty. These findings suggest that increased Kinaesthetic demands may slightly impair immediate recall, independent of perceived workload or mental effort. Importantly, the manipulation of touch alone does not appear to influence information retention. The study contributes to understanding the nuanced relationship between embodied interactions and cognitive outcomes, with implications for designing sensor-based multimodal learning environments.

</details>


### [15] [Wire Your Way: Hardware-Contextualized Guidance and In-situ Tests for Personalized Circuit Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05085)
*Punn Lertjaturaphat,Jungwoo Rhee,Jaewon You,Andrea Bianchi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了一个支持个性化电路构建和调试的原型平台，通过增强型面包板提供电路感知、实时硬件重配置和原位电路验证功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统教程和工具包采用逐步指导的方式，与创客在电路构建、问题评估和调试方面的个性化偏好存在不匹配。创客有独特的电路构建偏好和问题调试方式，需要更灵活的支持系统。

Method: 开发了一个电路感知的增强型面包板原型平台，支持通过情境化指导进行实时硬件重配置，并通过交互式测试进行原位电路验证。通过可用性研究(N=12)评估系统效果。

Result: 可用性研究(N=12)表明，创客能够利用电路感知指导和调试功能来支持个性化的构建模式，系统有效解决了传统教程与创客个性化需求之间的不匹配问题。

Conclusion: 提出的原型平台通过电路感知、实时重配置和原位验证功能，成功支持了创客的个性化电路构建和调试需求，为创客教育工具设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: The increasing popularity of microcontroller platforms like Arduino enables diverse end-user developers to participate in circuit prototyping. Traditionally, follow-along tutorials serve as an essential learning method for makers, and in fact, several prior toolkits leveraged this format as a way to engage new makers. However, literature and our formative study (N=12) show that makers have unique preferences regarding the construction of their circuits and idiosyncratic ways to assess and debug problems, which contrasts with the step-by-step instructional nature of tutorials and those systems leveraging this method. To address this mismatch, we present a prototyping platform that supports personalized circuit construction and debugging. Our system utilizes an augmented breadboard, which is circuit-aware and supports on-the-fly hardware reconfiguration via contextualized guidance and in-situ circuit validation through interactive tests. Through a usability study (N=12), we demonstrate how makers leverage circuit-aware guidance and debugging to support individual building patterns.

</details>


### [16] [Not All Trust is the Same: Effects of Decision Workflow and Explanations in Human-AI Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05229)
*Laura Spillner,Rachel Ringe,Robert Porzel,Rainer Malaka*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了AI辅助决策中信任校准问题，比较了单步与双步决策流程、解释机制、用户领域知识和AI经验对信任报告和依赖行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助决策中的核心挑战是实现合理、校准良好的信任。既要防止过度信任（接受错误的AI建议），也要防止信任不足（拒绝正确的建议）。先前研究在决策流程设计（单步vs双步）和信任测量方式（自我报告vs行为依赖）上存在差异，需要系统研究这些因素及其交互作用。

Method: 研究考察了三种因素的效应和交互作用：(a)决策流程类型（单步设置：用户立即看到AI建议；双步设置：用户需先提交初步决策），(b)解释机制的存在，(c)用户的领域知识和先前AI经验。比较了报告的信任、依赖行为（同意率和切换率）以及过度依赖。

Result: 结果显示：1) 没有证据表明双步设置能减少过度依赖；2) 决策流程不直接影响自我报告的信任，但与领域知识和解释机制存在交叉交互效应，表明解释机制的效果可能无法在不同工作流程设置中普遍适用；3) 报告的信任和依赖行为是不同的构念，应在AI辅助决策中分别评估。

Conclusion: 在AI辅助决策中，报告的信任和依赖行为应被视为不同的构念进行独立评估。决策流程设置（单步vs双步）本身并不直接影响信任或减少过度依赖，但其与用户特征（如领域知识）和系统特征（如解释机制）的交互作用需要仔细考虑。解释机制的效果可能因工作流程设置而异，强调了情境因素在AI信任研究中的重要性。

Abstract: A central challenge in AI-assisted decision making is achieving warranted, well-calibrated trust. Both overtrust (accepting incorrect AI recommendations) and undertrust (rejecting correct advice) should be prevented. Prior studies differ in the design of the decision workflow - whether users see the AI suggestion immediately (1-step setup) or have to submit a first decision beforehand (2-step setup) -, and in how trust is measured - through self-reports or as behavioral trust, that is, reliance. We examined the effects and interactions of (a) the type of decision workflow, (b) the presence of explanations, and (c) users' domain knowledge and prior AI experience. We compared reported trust, reliance (agreement rate and switch rate), and overreliance. Results showed no evidence that a 2-step setup reduces overreliance. The decision workflow also did not directly affect self-reported trust, but there was a crossover interaction effect with domain knowledge and explanations, suggesting that the effects of explanations alone may not generalize across workflow setups. Finally, our findings confirm that reported trust and reliance behavior are distinct constructs that should be evaluated separately in AI-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [17] [AttentiveLearn: Personalized Post-Lecture Support for Gaze-Aware Immersive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05324)
*Shi Liu,Martin Feick,Linus Bierhoff,Alexander Maedche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AttentiveLearn：基于VR课堂眼动追踪的个性化移动学习助手，通过注意力分布生成个性化测验，提升学习动机和参与度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式学习环境（如VR虚拟教室）虽然提供独特学习体验，但缺乏有效的学习者支持。现有HCI研究主要关注课中支持，而对课后支持的研究不足，而课后支持对维持学习动机、参与度和学习成果至关重要。

Method: 开发AttentiveLearn学习生态系统：通过VR课堂中的眼动追踪推断学习者注意力分布，基于此在移动学习助手上生成个性化测验。采用为期四周的实地研究，36名大学生参与贝叶斯数据分析课程。

Result: AttentiveLearn提高了学习者报告的学习动机和参与度，但学习成果提升的证据不明确。轶事证据表明某些参与者的注意力随时间有所改善。

Conclusion: 研究为沉浸式学习系统的个性化课后支持提供了实证见解和设计启示，强调基于注意力追踪的个性化支持对提升学习动机和参与度的有效性。

Abstract: Immersive learning environments such as virtual classrooms in Virtual Reality (VR) offer learners unique learning experiences, yet providing effective learner support remains a challenge. While prior HCI research has explored in-lecture support for immersive learning, little research has been conducted to provide post-lecture support, despite being critical for sustained motivation, engagement, and learning outcomes. To address this, we present AttentiveLearn, a learning ecosystem that generates personalized quizzes on a mobile learning assistant based on learners' attention distribution inferred using eye-tracking in VR lectures. We evaluated the system in a four-week field study with 36 university students attending lectures on Bayesian data analysis. AttentiveLearn improved learners' reported motivation and engagement, without conclusive evidence of learning gains. Meanwhile, anecdotal evidence suggested improvements in attention for certain participants over time. Based on our findings of the field study, we provide empirical insights and design implications for personalized post-lecture support for immersive learning systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [18] [Fusions of One-Variable First-Order Modal Logics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04512)
*Roman Kontchakov,Dmitry Shkatov,Frank Wolter*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 研究单变量一阶模态逻辑独立融合的保持性结果，发现无等词时克里普克完全性和可判定性在全局与局部后承关系下均保持，但带等词和非刚性常元时则不保持，有限模型性仅在局部情况下保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单变量一阶模态逻辑融合时各种逻辑性质（克里普克完全性、可判定性、有限模型性）的保持性问题，特别关注等词和非刚性常元的影响。

Method: 采用独立融合方法，分析无等词和有等词两种情况，使用丢番图方程编码技术证明某些性质不保持，并将单变量模态逻辑融合视为共享S5模态的命题模态逻辑融合。

Result: 无等词时克里普克完全性和可判定性在全局与局部后承关系下均保持（扩展域和常域语义）；带等词和非刚性常元时这些性质不保持；有限模型性仅在局部情况下保持。

Conclusion: 单变量一阶模态逻辑融合的性质保持性取决于是否包含等词和非刚性常元，为这类逻辑融合提供了系统的保持性分析框架。

Abstract: We investigate preservation results for the independent fusion of one-variable first-order modal logics. We show that, without equality, Kripke completeness and decidability of the global and local consequence relation are preserved, under both expanding and constant domain semantics. By contrast, Kripke completeness and decidability are not preserved for fusions with equality and non-rigid constants (or, equivalently, counting up to one), again for the global and local consequence and under both expanding and constant domain semantics. This result is shown by encoding Diophantine equations. Even without equality, the finite model property is only preserved in the local case. Finally, we view fusions of one-variable modal logics as fusions of propositional modal logics sharing an S5 modality and provide a general sufficient condition for transfer of Kripke completeness and decidability (but not of finite model property).

</details>

*Ralph Sarkis,Fabio Zanasi*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文从范畴论角度研究相对熵，将其视为随机矩阵范畴的定量富集，针对Kronecker积和直和两种幺半结构，给出了KL散度和Rényi散度的完整公理化体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对熵是概率分布间距离的基本类别，在概率论、统计学和机器学习中有广泛应用。本文旨在从范畴论视角研究相对熵，将其视为随机矩阵范畴的定量富集，为KL散度和Rényi散度提供范畴论基础。

Method: 采用范畴论方法，将相对熵视为随机矩阵范畴的定量富集。研究随机矩阵的两种自然幺半结构：Kronecker积和直和。使用定量幺半代数框架，通过带有定量方程的弦图语言，构建公理化理论。

Result: 主要成果是针对Kronecker积和直和两种幺半结构，分别给出了Kullback-Leibler散度以及更一般的任意阶Rényi散度的完整公理化体系。

Conclusion: 该工作为相对熵提供了范畴论基础，通过定量幺半代数框架和图形化语言，为KL散度和Rényi散度建立了严格的公理化理论，为概率论和机器学习中的信息度量提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Relative entropy is a fundamental class of distances between probability distributions, with widespread applications in probability theory, statistics, and machine learning. In this work, we study relative entropy from a categorical perspective, viewing it as a quantitative enrichment of categories of stochastic matrices. We consider two natural monoidal structures on stochastic matrices, given by the Kronecker product and the direct sum. Our main results are complete axiomatisations of Kullback-Leibler divergence and, more generally, of Rényi divergences of arbitrary order, for each such structure. Our axiomatic theories are formulated within the framework of quantitative monoidal algebra, using a graphical language of string diagrams enriched with quantitative equations.

</details>


### [20] [Modal Fragments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05055)
*Nick Bezhanishvili,Balder ten Cate,Arunavo Ganguly,Arne Meier*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文综述了命题逻辑和模态逻辑基限制片段的研究方法，重点探讨表达能力与计算复杂度如何依赖于允许的算子，并整合了历史上独立的两条研究路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在系统化研究命题逻辑和模态逻辑的基限制片段，建立统一的分析框架。动机包括：1) 命题逻辑片段已有完善理论（Post格），可作为概念模板；2) 模态逻辑片段存在两条独立研究路线需要整合；3) 需要系统分析表达能力与计算复杂度的关系；4) 探索可教性和精确可学习性等应用问题。

Method: 研究方法包括：1) 以Post格作为命题逻辑片段分类的理论基础；2) 提出参数化模态片段的一般框架，其中模态片段由任意模态公式定义的"连接词"基参数化；3) 定义更易处理的简单模态片段，由布尔函数加选定模态算子参数化；4) 应用Post格方法进行系统可判定性和二分结果分析；5) 收集并扩展命题片段和简单模态片段的可教性和精确可学习性结果。

Result: 研究结果包括：1) 建立了命题逻辑片段通过布尔克隆组织的系统分类；2) 整合了模态片段的两条历史独立研究路线；3) 为简单模态片段实现了系统的可判定性和二分结果；4) 收集并扩展了命题片段和简单模态片段的可教性和精确可学习性结果；5) 识别了若干开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了命题逻辑和模态逻辑基限制片段的统一分析框架。结论包括：1) Post格为命题逻辑片段提供了有效的组织原则；2) 参数化方法能够统一处理模态片段；3) 简单模态片段具有更好的可处理性；4) 可教性和可学习性分析展示了理论的实际应用价值；5) 识别了多个值得进一步研究的开放问题。

Abstract: We survey systematic approaches to basis-restricted fragments of propositional logic and modal logics, with an emphasis on how expressive power and computational complexity depend on the allowed operators. The propositional case is well-established and serves as a conceptual template: Post's lattice organizes fragments via Boolean clones and supports complexity classifications for standard reasoning tasks. For modal fragments, we then bring together two historically independent lines of investigation: a general framework where modal fragments are parameterized by a basis of "connectives" defined by arbitrary modal formulas (initially proposed and studied by logicians such as Kuznetsov and Ratsa in the 1970s), and the more tractable class of what we call simple modal fragments parameterized by Boolean functions plus selected modal operators, where Post-lattice methods enable systematic decidability and dichotomy results. Along the way, we collect and extend results on teachability and exact learnability from examples for both propositional fragments and simple modal fragments, and we conclude by identifying several open problems.

</details>


### [21] [The Complexity of the Constructive Master Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05131)
*Sofía Santiago-Fernández,David Fernández-Duque,Joost J. Joosten*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文引入了构造性主模态逻辑CK*和WK*，证明了它们的EXPTIME完备性和指数大小有限模型性质，并解决了Afshari等人的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展基本构造性模态逻辑CK和Wijesekera风格逻辑WK，通过引入语义定义的主模态逻辑来研究这些系统的计算复杂性和模型性质。

Method: 使用翻译技术将CK*和WK*与PDL（命题动态逻辑）片段建立联系，通过这种对应关系分析逻辑性质。

Result: 证明了CK*和WK*都是EXPTIME完备的，具有指数大小的有限模型性质；对于无钻石片段也确立了EXPTIME完备性，解决了Afshari等人的猜想。

Conclusion: 构造性主模态逻辑具有可判定的EXPTIME复杂度，并能嵌入CS4和WS4逻辑，表明这些系统的有效性判定问题也在EXPTIME内。

Abstract: We introduce the semantically-defined constructive master-modality logics $\sf CK^*$ and $\sf WK^*$, extending the basic constructive modal logic $\sf CK$ and the Wijesekera-style logic $\sf WK$ obtained by impossing infallibility. Using translations between our logics and fragments of $\sf PDL$, we show that both $\sf CK^*$ and $\sf WK^*$ are EXPTIME-complete and admit an exponential-size finite model property. In particular, for their diamond-free fragment, also studied by Afshari et al. and Celoni, we establish EXPTIME-completeness, thereby settling the conjecture of Afshari et al.
  As an application, we embed $\sf CS4$ and $\sf WS4$ into the master-modality logics, showing that their validity problems are in EXPTIME.

</details>


### [22] [Constraint Learning for Non-confluent Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05258)
*Michael Rawson,Clemens Eisenhofer,Laura Kovács*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 在非合流表计算法中采用约束学习来减少回溯，同时保持完备性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合流表计算法（如连接表计算法）中的证明搜索存在过度回溯问题，而简单的回溯限制会导致不完备性

Method: 在经典一阶连接计算法中采用约束学习来减少回溯，迭代精化连接驱动搜索的初始约束学习语言

Result: 约束学习方法在实践中显著减少了回溯，同时保持了完备性

Conclusion: 该方法可能对其他非合流表计算法中的证明搜索也有用

Abstract: Proof search in non-confluent tableau calculi, such as the connection tableau calculus, suffers from excess backtracking, but simple restrictions on backtracking are incomplete. We adopt constraint learning to reduce backtracking in the classical first-order connection calculus, while retaining completeness. An initial constraint learning language for connection-driven search is iteratively refined to greatly reduce backtracking in practice. The approach may be useful for proof search in other non-confluent tableau calculi.

</details>

### [23] [Towards a B+-tree with Fluctuation-Free Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04785)
*Lu Xing,Walid G. Aref*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: FFBtree是一种B+树插入算法，通过预分裂关键节点消除分裂传播，将插入操作的I/O波动从3倍降低到恒定，实现性能可预测性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据库管理系统需要性能可预测性，因为索引维护可能引发罕见但严重的I/O峰值。传统B/B+树中节点分裂传播导致单次插入成本从H+1到3H+1 I/O波动，性能退化近3倍

Method: 引入安全节点和关键节点的概念来捕捉不可避免的分裂情况，提出FFBtree插入算法，通过预分裂关键节点确保从根到叶的遍历中最多遇到一个必须分裂的关键节点，从而消除分裂传播

Result: 模拟索引实验显示FFBtree通过消除分裂传播限制了I/O波动，相比传统基线持续减少插入峰值；真实索引实验确认了可比改进

Conclusion: FFBtree算法通过预分裂关键节点实现了波动自由的性能，解决了B+树插入操作中的性能可预测性问题，同时通过高效的关键节点元数据管理和乐观锁耦合支持并发

Abstract: Performance predictability is critical for modern DBMSs because index maintenance can trigger rare but severe I/O spikes. In a B or B+-tree with height H, node split propagation means the cost of a single insert can vary from H + 1 to 3H + 1 I/Os when splits reach the root, nearly a three times degradation. We formalize performance fluctuation as the gap between best- and worst-case insert behavior and introduce the notions of safe and critical nodes to capture when splits become unavoidable. We introduce the FFBtree, a B+-tree insert algorithm that preemptively splits some critical nodes, and prove that when navigating from root to leaf the insert algorithm will encounter at most one critical node that must be split, ensuring no split propagation can occur and producing fluctuation-free performance. Our implementation maintains critical-node metadata efficiently and integrates with optimistic lock coupling for concurrency. Experiments with simulated indexes show the FFBtree caps I/O fluctuation by eliminating split propagation and consistently reduces insert spikes compared to conventional baselines, and real-index experiments confirm comparable improvements.

</details>


### [24] [Beyond Linear LLM Invocation: An Efficient and Effective Semantic Filter Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04799)
*Nan Hou,Kangfei Zhao,Jiadong Xie,Jeffrey Xu Yu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CSV框架通过聚类-采样-投票方法将语义过滤的LLM调用复杂度从线性降低到亚线性，同时提供错误保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义过滤操作需要逐元组线性扫描，导致高昂的延迟和token成本，现有优化方法未能突破线性LLM调用障碍

Method: 提出CSV（聚类-采样-投票）框架：1）将元组嵌入语义聚类；2）采样小规模子集进行LLM评估；3）通过两种投票策略推断聚类级标签（UniVote均匀聚合，SimVote基于语义相似度加权）；4）对模糊聚类触发重新聚类以确保鲁棒性

Result: 在真实数据集上，CSV相比最先进方法将LLM调用次数减少了1.28-355倍，同时在准确率和F1分数上保持相当的有效性

Conclusion: CSV框架成功解决了语义过滤中的线性扫描问题，实现了亚线性复杂度的LLM调用，为大规模语料库的语义查询处理提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for semantic query processing over large corpora. A set of semantic operators derived from relational algebra has been proposed to provide a unified interface for expressing such queries, among which the semantic filter operator serves as a cornerstone. Given a table T with a natural language predicate e, for each tuple in the relation, the execution of a semantic filter proceeds by constructing an input prompt that combines the predicate e with its content, querying the LLM, and obtaining the binary decision. However, this tuple-by-tuple evaluation necessitates a complete linear scan of the table, incurring prohibitive latency and token costs. Although recent work has attempted to optimize semantic filtering, it still does not break the linear LLM invocation barriers. To address this, we propose Clustering-Sampling-Voting (CSV), a new framework that reduces LLM invocations to sublinear complexity while providing error guarantees. CSV embeds tuples into semantic clusters, samples a small subset for LLM evaluation, and infers cluster-level labels via two proposed voting strategies: UniVote, which aggregates labels uniformly, and SimVote, which weights votes by semantic similarity. Moreover, CSV triggers re-clustering on ambiguous clusters to ensure robustness across diverse datasets. The results conducted on real-world datasets demonstrate that CSV reduces the number of LLM calls by 1.28-355x compared to the state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining comparable effectiveness in terms of Accuracy and F1 score.

</details>


### [25] [Deterministic Preprocessing and Interpretable Fuzzy Banding for Cost-per-Student Reporting from Extracted Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04905)
*Shane Lee,Stella Ng*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 开发了一个确定性、基于规则的文件处理工作流，用于处理学术管理数据，计算成本-学生比率并添加模糊分级层，支持可重复计算和决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行政数据通常以电子表格形式交换，并在预算、工作量审查和治理讨论中被用作参考快照。当导出的工作簿成为此类决策的参考时，需要通过重新计算来验证数据转换的准确性。

Method: 实现了一个确定性、基于规则的文件工作流(cad_processor.py)，处理学术数据库导出工作簿，聚合包含成本的费用和学生数量，计算学科-年度和学院-年度总量，推导出成本-学生比率。添加了模糊分级层，使用左肩、三角和右肩函数计算成员权重，采用确定性平局处理机制。

Result: 生成包含四个工作表(处理摘要、趋势分析、报告、模糊分级)的处理后工作簿。处理摘要包含输入工作簿的SHA-256哈希值以支持快照匹配的重新计算。模糊分级将成本-学生比率标记为低、中、高三个等级。

Conclusion: 该工作流提供了可重复的计算框架，将模糊分级权重作为决策支持信号而非概率，通过工作示例和补充材料(声明-证据矩阵、可重复性说明、术语表)确保透明度和可验证性。

Abstract: Administrative extracts are often exchanged as spreadsheets and may be read as reports in their own right during budgeting, workload review, and governance discussions. When an exported workbook becomes the reference snapshot for such decisions, the transformation can be checked by recomputation against a clearly identified input.
  A deterministic, rule-governed, file-based workflow is implemented in cad_processor.py. The script ingests a Casual Academic Database (CAD) export workbook and aggregates inclusive on-costs and student counts into subject-year and school-year totals, from which it derives cost-per-student ratios. It writes a processed workbook with four sheets: Processing Summary (run record and counters), Trend Analysis (schoolyear cost-per-student matrix), Report (wide subject-level table), and Fuzzy Bands (per-year anchors, membership weights, and band labels). The run record includes a SHA-256 hash of the input workbook bytes to support snapshot-matched recomputation.
  For within-year interpretation, the workflow adds a simple fuzzy banding layer that labels finite, positive school-year cost-per-student values as Low, Medium, or High. The per-year anchors are the minimum, median, and maximum of the finite, positive ratios. Membership weights are computed using left-shoulder, triangular, and right-shoulder functions, with deterministic tie-breaking in a fixed priority order (Medium, then Low, then High). These weights are treated as decision-support signals rather than probabilities.
  A worked example provides a reproducible calculation of a band assignment from the reported anchors and ratios. Supplementary material includes a claim-to-evidence matrix, a reproducibility note, and a short glossary that links selected statements to code and workbook artefacts.

</details>


### [26] [FluxSieve: Unifying Streaming and Analytical Data Planes for Scalable Cloud Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04937)
*Adriano Vogel,Sören Henning,Otmar Ertl*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: FluxSieve：一种在数据摄取路径中嵌入轻量级流内预计算和过滤层的统一架构，将传统拉取式查询处理与推送式流处理相结合，显著提升高并发查询性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据平台在高并发和计算密集型查询下难以提供稳健性能，特别是在大规模可观测性平台处理高容量、高速数据记录时。重复的昂贵过滤查询在查询时带来大量计算和存储开销。

Method: 提出FluxSieve统一架构，在数据摄取路径中嵌入轻量级流内预计算和过滤层，避免在专用流处理框架中运行查询的复杂性。包括：(1)通过流内过滤和记录丰富化统一流式和分析数据平面的基础架构；(2)设计支持并发评估和动态更新过滤规则的可扩展多模式匹配机制；(3)将该摄取时处理与Apache Pinot（RTOLAP引擎）和DuckDB（嵌入式分析数据库）集成。

Result: 在不同系统、查询类型和性能指标上的综合实验评估显示，查询性能提升可达数量级，同时仅带来可忽略的额外存储和极低计算开销。

Conclusion: FluxSieve通过将流内预计算和过滤嵌入数据摄取路径，有效统一了流式和分析数据处理，显著提升了高并发查询性能，同时保持了低开销。

Abstract: Despite many advances in query optimization, indexing techniques, and data storage, modern data platforms still face difficulties in delivering robust query performance under high concurrency and computationally intensive queries. This challenge is particularly pronounced in large-scale observability platforms handling high-volume, high-velocity data records. For instance, recurrent, expensive filtering queries at query time impose substantial computational and storage overheads in the analytical data plane. In this paper, we propose FluxSieve, a unified architecture that reconciles traditional pull-based query processing with push-based stream processing by embedding a lightweight in-stream precomputation and filtering layer directly into the data ingestion path. This avoids the complexity and operational burden of running queries in dedicated stream processing frameworks. Concretely, this work (i) introduces a foundational architecture that unifies streaming and analytical data planes via in-stream filtering and records enrichment, (ii) designs a scalable multi-pattern matching mechanism that supports concurrent evaluation and on-the-fly updates of filtering rules with minimal per-record overhead, (iii) demonstrates how to integrate this ingestion-time processing with two open-source analytical systems -- Apache Pinot as a Real-Time Online Analytical Processing (RTOLAP) engine and DuckDB as an embedded analytical database, and (iv) performs comprehensive experimental evaluation of our approach. Our evaluation across different systems, query types, and performance metrics shows up to orders-of-magnitude improvements in query performance at the cost of negligible additional storage and very low computational overhead.

</details>


### [27] [CRISP: Correlation-Resilient Indexing via Subspace Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05180)
*Dimitris Dimitropoulos,Achilleas Michalopoulos,Dimitrios Tsitsigkos,Nikos Mamoulis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CRISP：针对超高维ANN搜索的新型框架，通过相关性感知自适应策略和CSR索引结构，在保持召回率的同时显著降低预处理复杂度和内存消耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着学习表示维度增至数千维，现有ANN索引方法面临严重限制：图基方法（如HNSW）内存消耗过高且路由退化，随机量化方法（如RaBitQ、OPQ）预处理开销巨大

Method: 1）轻量级相关性感知自适应策略，仅在必要时重新分配方差；2）缓存友好的压缩稀疏行（CSR）索引结构；3）双模式查询引擎：保证模式（保持理论召回下界）和优化模式（基于排名的加权评分和提前终止）

Result: 在高达4096维的数据集上评估显示，CRISP实现了最先进的查询吞吐量、低构建成本和峰值内存效率

Conclusion: CRISP为超高维ANN搜索提供了一种高效解决方案，通过自适应策略和双模式查询引擎，在查询性能、内存效率和预处理成本之间取得良好平衡

Abstract: As the dimensionality of modern learned representations increases to thousands of dimensions, the state-of-the-art Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) indices exhibit severe limitations. Graph-based methods (e.g., HNSW) suffer from prohibitive memory consumption and routing degradation, while recent randomized quantization and learned rotation approaches (e.g., RaBitQ, OPQ) impose significant preprocessing overheads. We introduce CRISP, a novel framework designed for ANN search in very-high-dimensional spaces. Unlike rigid pipelines that apply expensive orthogonal rotations indiscriminately, CRISP employs a lightweight, correlation- aware adaptive strategy that redistributes variance only when necessary, effectively reducing the preprocessing complexity. We couple this adaptive mechanism with a cache-coherent Compressed Sparse Row (CSR) index structure. Furthermore, CRISP incorporates a multi-stage dual-mode query engine: a Guaranteed Mode that preserves rigorous theoretical lower bounds on recall, and an Optimized Mode that leverages rank-based weighted scoring and early termination to reduce query latency. Extensive evaluation on datasets of very high dimensionality (up to 4096) demonstrates that CRISP achieves state-of-the-art query throughput, low construction costs, and peak memory efficiency.

</details>

### [28] [iScript: A Domain-Adapted Large Language Model and Benchmark for Physical Design Tcl Script Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04476)
*Ning Xu,Zhaoyang Zhang,Senlin Shu,Lei Qi,Jiaqi Lv,Wensuo Wang,Tianhao Zhao,Chao Zhang,Zhaoliang Yang,Xiangyu Li,Zhaorui Su,Jingshan Li,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: iScript是一个针对Innovus Tcl脚本生成的领域自适应Qwen3-8B模型，通过多阶段数据合成和两阶段训练策略解决EDA领域数据稀缺问题，在专门基准测试中表现优于现有LLMs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代EDA流程严重依赖Tcl脚本，但通用大语言模型在该领域表现不佳，主要原因是：1) 极端数据稀缺；2) 领域特定语义；3) 物理设计对高可靠性的要求。需要专门解决方案来提升EDA脚本生成的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 1) 提出iScript-Bench基准，涵盖5个任务类别和3个难度级别；2) 开发多阶段数据合成管道，集成命令提取、静态检查、需求反推和思维链生成，产生10K个(需求, CoT, 脚本)数据对；3) 采用两阶段训练策略：领域自适应预训练+监督微调；4) 提出两步验证框架：静态语法验证+基于LLM的功能评估。

Result: 在iScript-Bench基准测试中，iScript在平均pass@k分数上优于当前最先进的大语言模型，证明了领域适应和数据合成方法在EDA脚本任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了针对特定领域（如EDA Tcl脚本生成）的领域适应和数据合成策略的有效性，为解决数据稀缺的专业领域LLM应用提供了可行方案，显著提升了脚本生成的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Modern EDA flows rely heavily on Tcl scripting, yet general LLMs perform poorly in this domain due to extreme data scarcity, domain-specific semantics, and the high reliability required in physical design. We present iScript, a domain-adapted Qwen3-8B model for Innovus Tcl script generation, and iScript-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark covering five task categories and three difficulty levels. To overcome the lack of training data, we introduce a multi-stage data synthesis pipeline that integrates command extraction, static linting, requirement back-inference, and Chain-of-Thought generation, producing a 10K-tuple (requirement, CoT, script) dataset. iScript is trained through a two-stage strategy combining domain-adaptive pretraining and supervised fine-tuning. To evaluate script correctness efficiently, we further propose a two-step verification framework consisting of static syntax verification and LLM-based functional evaluation. On our benchmark, iScript shows higher pass@k scores than currently state-of-the-art LLMs on average. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of domain adaptation and data synthesis for EDA scripting tasks.

</details>


### [29] [CLARC: C/C++ Benchmark for Robust Code Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04484)
*Kaicheng Wang,Liyan Huang,Weike Fang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CLARC是一个用于C/C++代码检索的基准测试，包含真实GitHub代码库构建的查询-代码对，通过LLM生成自然语言查询，并引入标识符匿名化、汇编编译等挑战性设置来测试模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索基准主要关注Python，缺乏对C/C++的支持，且很少测试模型在超越表面词汇线索之外的鲁棒性。需要构建能够有效评估代码检索模型在真实复杂场景下性能的基准。

Method: 开发自动化流水线从真实GitHub仓库构建数据集，确保代码可编译性，按依赖复杂度分类代码片段。使用LLM生成自然语言查询并通过人工评分和假设检验验证。引入标识符匿名化、编译为汇编和WebAssembly等挑战性设置来测试检索鲁棒性。

Result: 构建了包含1,245个评估对和5,472个训练对的CLARC基准。在六个最先进模型的评估中，在挑战性设置下检索效果显著下降，表明模型过度依赖词汇特征而非代码语义理解。

Conclusion: CLARC基准揭示了当前代码检索模型在C/C++环境下对词汇特征的过度依赖，缺乏真正的语义理解能力。该基准为评估和改进代码检索模型的鲁棒性提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Efficient code retrieval is critical for developer productivity, yet existing benchmarks largely focus on Python and rarely stress-test robustness beyond superficial lexical cues. To address the gap, we introduce an automated pipeline for code search datasets and present CLARC, a C/C++ benchmark built from real-world GitHub repositories. CLARC contains 1,245 query-code pairs for evaluation and 5,472 pairs for training. The benchmark incorporates LLM-generated natural language queries validated through rigorous human scoring and hypothesis testing. To analyze contextual requirements effectively, our pipeline starts by ensuring code compilability. It then categorizes code snippets by dependency complexity, distinguishing whether the code relies on custom-defined types or helper functions. The pipeline also enables CLARC to stress-test retrieval robustness by introducing challenging settings, including identifier anonymization and compilation to low-level languages like Assembly and WebAssembly. Under these conditions, our evaluation of six state-of-the-art models reveals sharp drops in retrieval effectiveness. The experimental results highlight the models' persistent reliance on lexical features rather than code semantic understanding. Our dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ClarcTeam/CLARC.

</details>


### [30] [Industrial Survey on Robustness Testing In Cyber Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04587)
*Christophe Ponsard,Abiola Paterne Chokki,Jean-François Daune*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对瓦隆尼亚地区工业界进行的关于信息物理系统（CPS）稳健性实践现状的调查，涵盖需求工程、系统设计、测试执行、故障模式和可用工具等方面，识别行业实践与前沿方法之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息物理系统在现代工业领域（制造、能源、交通、医疗等）中发挥着关键作用，其稳健性至关重要，因为故障可能导致重大的经济、运营和安全后果。需要了解工业界在CPS稳健性方面的实践现状，识别挑战和差距。

Method: 在瓦隆尼亚地区进行工业调查，覆盖多个行业领域，从需求工程、系统设计、测试执行、故障模式和可用工具等维度评估CPS稳健性实践。将调查结果与文献中的类似工业调查进行比较。

Result: 调查揭示了工业界对CPS稳健性的理解和应用现状，识别了行业实践与最先进方法论之间的关键挑战和差距。通过与文献中类似调查的比较，提供了更全面的行业实践视角。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解工业界CPS稳健性实践提供了实证基础，揭示了当前实践与理论方法之间的差距，为改进CPS稳健性工程实践和工具开发提供了方向。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) play a critical role in modern industrial domains, including manufacturing, energy, transportation, and healthcare, where they enable automation, optimization, and real-time decision-making. Ensuring the robustness of these systems is paramount, as failures can have significant economic, operational, and safety consequences. This paper present findings from an industrial survey conducted in Wallonia, covering a wide range of sectors, to assess the current state of practice in CPS robustness. It investigates robustness from how it is understood and applied in relationship with requirements engineering, system design, test execution, failure modes, and available tools. It identifies key challenges and gaps between industry practices and state-of-the-art methodologies. Additionally, it compares our findings with similar industrial surveys from the literature.

</details>


### [31] [Behaviour Driven Development Scenario Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04729)
*Amila Rathnayake,Mojtaba Shahin,Golnoush Abaei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 评估GPT-4、Claude 3和Gemini在自动化BDD场景生成中的表现，发现Claude 3在人工和LLM评估中得分最高，而GPT-4在文本语义相似度上表现最佳。不同模型对提示技术有特定偏好，输入质量对生成效果有决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型在行为驱动开发场景生成任务中的实际表现，比较不同模型（GPT-4、Claude 3、Gemini）的生成质量，探索最优的提示技术和参数配置，为软件开发中的自动化测试生成提供实践指导。

Method: 构建包含500个用户故事、需求描述及对应BDD场景的数据集，采用多维评估框架：文本相似度指标、语义相似度指标、LLM-based评估（包括DeepSeek）和人工专家评估。测试不同提示技术（零样本、思维链、少样本）和参数配置（温度、top_p）的影响。

Result: Claude 3在人工专家和LLM评估中得分最高，GPT-4在文本和语义相似度指标上表现最佳。DeepSeek评估与人工判断相关性最强。不同模型对提示技术有特定偏好：GPT-4适合零样本，Claude 3适合思维链，Gemini适合少样本。详细需求描述比单独用户故事能产生更高质量的BDD场景。温度设为0、top_p设为1.0时所有模型生成质量最高。

Conclusion: LLM在自动化BDD场景生成中具有实用价值，但模型选择、提示技术和输入质量都显著影响生成效果。Claude 3在整体质量评估中表现最优，LLM-based评估（特别是DeepSeek）可作为人工评估的有效替代。详细的需求描述是高质量BDD场景生成的关键前提。

Abstract: This paper presents an evaluation of three LLMs, GPT-4, Claude 3, and Gemini, for automated Behaviour-Driven Development (BDD) scenarios generation. To support this evaluation, we constructed a dataset of 500 user stories, requirement descriptions, and their corresponding BDD scenarios, drawn from four proprietary software products. We assessed the quality of BDD scenarios generated by LLMs using a multidimensional evaluation framework encompassing text and semantic similarity metrics, LLM-based evaluation, and human expert assessment. Our findings reveal that although GPT-4 achieves higher scores in text and semantic similarity metrics, Claude 3 produces scenarios rated highest by both human experts and LLM-based evaluators. LLM-based evaluators, particularly DeepSeek, show a stronger correlation with human judgment than with text similarity and semantic similarity metrics. The effectiveness of prompting techniques is model-specific: GPT-4 performs best with zero-shot, Claude 3 benefits from chain-of-thought reasoning, and Gemini achieves optimal results with few-shot examples. Input quality determines the effectiveness of BDD scenario generation: detailed requirement descriptions alone yield high-quality scenarios, whereas user stories alone yield low-quality scenarios. Our experiments indicate that setting temperature to 0 and top_p to 1.0 produced the highest-quality BDD scenarios across all models.

</details>


### [32] [Public Sector Open Source Program Offices - Archetypes for how to Grow (Common) Institutional Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04891)
*Johan Linåker,Astor Nummelin Carlberg,Ciaran O'Riordan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了公共部门组织如何通过开源项目办公室（OSPOs）来促进开源软件的采用、开发和协作，识别了六种OSPO原型并提供了政策建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件是数字基础设施的关键组成部分，对欧洲公共部门数字化转型至关重要。研究旨在探索如何通过组织支持功能（如OSPOs）来促进公共部门对开源软件的采用、开发和协作。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对16个公共部门OSPO案例的18位代表进行访谈调查，通过交叉分析和分类识别出六种OSPO原型，并通过两个后续焦点小组验证和丰富研究结果。

Result: 研究识别出六种不同的OSPO原型，揭示了它们的组织结构、职责和对开源软件采用的贡献。这些原型及相关政策建议为公共部门组织设计适合自身背景、资源和政策目标的OSPO提供了指导。

Conclusion: 研究结果深化了对OSPO作为促进开源软件采用战略举措的理解，为公共部门组织和政策制定者提供了实用指导，帮助他们利用开源软件实现战略目标、促进数字主权、推动经济增长，并提高数字服务的互操作性和质量。

Abstract: Context: Open Source Software (OSS) is a crucial component of over 90\% of digital infrastructure underpinning industry and public digital services, facilitating collaborative software development and dissemination. Its significance in the European public sector has been emphasised through various Ministerial Declarations, highlighting its potential to accelerate digitalisation, transform businesses, and foster a digitally skilled population. Research Aim: This study aims to explore how the adoption, development, and collaboration on OSS can be enabled through organisational support functions or centres of competency, also known as Open Source Programme Offices (OSPOs) within Public Sector Organisations (PSOs) in the European Union, Norway, Liechtenstein, and Iceland. Methodology: A qualitative research approach was adopted, involving an interview survey of 18 OSPO representatives across 16 cases of public-sector OSPOs. These cases were cross-analysed and categorised into six OSPO archetypes. The findings were validated and enriched through two follow-up focus groups that included earlier interviewees and additional experts. Results: The study identified six distinct OSPO archetypes, providing insights into their organisational structures, responsibilities, and contributions to OSS adoption. The archetypes, along with policy recommendations, offer guidance on how PSOs can design their own OSPOs, taking into account their specific context, resources, and policy goals. Conclusions: The findings enhance the understanding of OSPOs as strategic endeavours aimed at promoting OSS adoption. The study offers practical guidance for PSOs and policymakers on leveraging OSS to achieve strategic objectives, foster digital sovereignty, drive economic growth, and improve the interoperability and quality of digital services.

</details>


### [33] [Why Do You Contribute to Stack Overflow? Understanding Cross-Cultural Motivations and Usage Patterns before the Age of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05043)
*Sherlock A. Licorish,Elijah Zolduoarrati,Tony Savarimuthu,Rashina Hoda,Ronnie De Souza Santos,Pankajeshwara Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究分析了Stack Overflow贡献者在不同国家文化背景下的参与动机差异，发现美国用户更倾向于自我推广，中国用户更注重学习导向，而详细个人资料的用户更可能参与广告和社交活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM时代，社区问答平台数据被用于训练模型，理解贡献者参与动机对维持知识共享生态系统至关重要。目前对不同国家文化背景下贡献者动机差异的研究有限，需要探索这些差异以促进软件工程领域的跨文化参与。

Method: 结合定性内容分析和定量语言分析，对美国、中国和俄罗斯的Stack Overflow用户数据进行研究。采用演绎内容分析法识别17个动机类别，并通过相关性分析探究陈述动机与平台活动之间的关联。

Result: 贡献者主要受广告机会和利他性解决问题欲望驱动。美国贡献者表现出更强的自我推广行为，中国贡献者则显示出更强的学习导向参与。相关性分析表明，拥有更详细个人资料的用户倾向于参与广告和社交活动，而学习导向用户则保持最低限度的自我展示。

Conclusion: 理解不同文化背景下贡献者动机的差异可以为增强软件工程领域跨文化参与的策略提供信息，有助于设计更有效的平台激励机制和社区管理方法。

Abstract: Understanding motivations of contributors for participating in community question and answer platforms is crucial for sustaining knowledge-sharing ecosystem, which is necessary to advance the discipline while also ensuring its longevity. This is particularly necessary in the age of LLMs, where data from such portals are used to train these models. Limited insights exist regarding how motivations of contributors vary across different national cultures. This research investigates Stack Overflow contributor motivations, analysing regional differences and relations to platform activity. We combined qualitative content analysis of Stack Overflow profiles with quantitative linguistic analysis of data from the United States, China, and Russia. Using deductive content analysis, we identified 17 motivational categories. We applied correlation analysis to identify associations between stated motivations and platform activities. Contributors are primarily motivated by advertising opportunities and altruistic problem solving desires. American contributors demonstrated stronger self promotional behaviours while Chinese contributors exhibited greater learning oriented engagement. Our correlation analysis showed that those with more detailed profiles tend to engage in advertising and social activities, while learning oriented users maintain minimal self presentation. Understanding these variations can inform strategies for enhancing cross cultural participation in software engineering.

</details>


### [34] [A Benchmarking Framework for Model Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05250)
*Philipp-Lorenz Glaser,Lola Burgueño,Dominik Bork*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出用于模型驱动工程中软件模型数据集的基准测试框架，解决现有数据集质量不一、可比性差和可复现性弱的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MDE领域的实证研究和基于LLM的研究依赖软件模型数据集，但这些数据集通常是临时收集或创建的，缺乏质量保证，导致结果可比性差、数据集质量不明确、可复现性弱且存在潜在偏差

Method: 提出模型数据集的基准测试框架，通过系统化测量数据集的质量、代表性和对特定任务的适用性来评估数据集本身。开发MDE基准平台，提供统一基础设施，使用定义的准则和指标跨语言和格式系统评估和比较软件模型数据集

Result: 提出了一个系统化的数据集评估框架，能够对软件模型数据集进行标准化质量评估，提高研究结果的可比性和可复现性

Conclusion: 需要将软件模型数据集视为一等公民进行系统评估，提出的基准测试框架和平台能够解决当前MDE研究中数据集质量不一的问题，促进更可靠和可比较的研究结果

Abstract: Empirical and LLM-based research in model-driven engineering increasingly relies on datasets of software models, for instance, to train or evaluate machine learning techniques for modeling support. These datasets have a significant impact on solution performance; hence, they should be treated and assessed as first-class artifacts. However, such datasets are typically collected or created ad hoc and without guarantees of their quality for the specific task for which they are used. This limits the comparability of results between studies, obscures dataset quality and representativeness, and leads to weak reproducibility and potential bias. In this work, we propose a benchmarking framework for model datasets (i.e., benchmarking the dataset itself). Benchmarking datasets involves systematically measuring their quality, representativeness, and suitability for specific tasks. To this end, we propose a Benchmark Platform for MDE that provides a unified infrastructure for systematically assessing and comparing datasets of software models across languages and formats, using defined criteria and metrics.

</details>


### [35] [A framework for assessing the capabilities of code generation of constraint domain-specific languages with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05278)
*David Delgado,Lola Burgueño,Robert Clarisó*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估LLM生成领域特定语言(DSL)代码能力的通用框架，并应用于约束语言OCL和Alloy，与Python对比发现LLM在通用语言上表现更好，同时探讨了代码修复、多尝试等改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在支持软件开发任务方面表现出色，但在处理不太流行的编程语言（如领域特定语言DSL）时效果显著下降。需要系统评估LLM从文本规范生成DSL代码的能力，以了解其局限性和改进方向。

Method: 提出一个通用的评估框架，从代码格式良好性和正确性两个维度评估LLM生成的DSL代码。将该框架应用于约束语言（OCL和Alloy），并与通用编程语言Python进行比较。实验考虑了上下文窗口大小、代码修复、多尝试生成、提示模板选择等多个维度。

Result: 实验结果显示：1) LLM在Python上的表现普遍优于OCL和Alloy；2) 具有较小上下文窗口的开源LLM可能无法生成约束相关代码，因为需要同时处理约束及其定义的领域模型；3) 代码修复和多次尝试等改进方法可以提高生成代码质量；4) 提示模板选择对结果影响较小。

Conclusion: 提出的评估框架可以系统分析LLM生成DSL代码的多个维度，帮助确定特定任务最有效的代码生成设置。研究揭示了LLM在处理DSL时的局限性，并提供了改进方向，如使用代码修复和多尝试生成策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be used to support software development tasks, e.g., through code completion or code generation. However, their effectiveness drops significantly when considering less popular programming languages such as domain-specific languages (DSLs). In this paper, we propose a generic framework for evaluating the capabilities of LLMs generating DSL code from textual specifications. The generated code is assessed from the perspectives of well-formedness and correctness. This framework is applied to a particular type of DSL, constraint languages, focusing our experiments on OCL and Alloy and comparing their results to those achieved for Python, a popular general-purpose programming language. Experimental results show that, in general, LLMs have better performance for Python than for OCL and Alloy. LLMs with smaller context windows such as open-source LLMs may be unable to generate constraint-related code, as this requires managing both the constraint and the domain model where it is defined. Moreover, some improvements to the code generation process such as code repair (asking an LLM to fix incorrect code) or multiple attempts (generating several candidates for each coding task) can improve the quality of the generated code. Meanwhile, other decisions like the choice of a prompt template have less impact. All these dimensions can be systematically analyzed using our evaluation framework, making it possible to decide the most effective way to set up code generation for a particular type of task.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>
