Method: 基于d维广义Kuramoto模型开发新聚类算法，该模型能有效处理单位球面S^{d-1}上的数据点，考虑了球面的几何特性。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上验证了方法的有效性，与传统聚类方法相比，新方法在聚类准确度方面达到相似或更好的结果。

Conclusion: 基于广义Kuramoto模型的聚类算法是针对单位超球面数据的一种有效方法，能够更好地利用球面几何结构，在多个应用领域具有实用价值。

Abstract: Clustering on the unit hypersphere is a fundamental problem in various fields, with applications ranging from gene expression analysis to text and image classification. Traditional clustering methods are not always suitable for unit sphere data, as they do not account for the geometric structure of the sphere. We introduce a novel algorithm for clustering data represented as points on the unit sphere $\mathbf{S}^{d-1}$. Our method is based on the $d$-dimensional generalized Kuramoto model. The effectiveness of the introduced method is demonstrated on synthetic and real-world datasets. Results are compared with some of the traditional clustering methods, showing that our method achieves similar or better results in terms of clustering accuracy.

</details>


### [77] [Aura: Universal Multi-dimensional Exogenous Integration for Aviation Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05092)
*Jiafeng Lin,Mengren Zheng,Simeng Ye,Yuxuan Wang,Huan Zhang,Yuhui Liu,Zhongyi Pei,Jianmin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Aura是一个通用框架，通过三部分编码机制将异质外部信息按交互模式组织并编码到时间序列模型中，在航空维护场景中显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业应用中时间序列预测需要整合多样化的外生因素，这些因素通常是多维甚至多模态的，存在异质交互关系，而单模态时间序列模型难以有效捕捉这些复杂关系。

Method: 提出Aura框架，根据外生因素与目标时间序列的交互模式，采用定制的三部分编码机制将异质特征嵌入到成熟的时间序列模型中，实现非序列上下文的无缝集成。

Result: 在中国南方航空公司三年大规模工业数据集（涵盖波音777和空客A320机队）上的广泛实验表明，Aura在所有基线方法中始终达到最先进的性能，并展现出卓越的适应性。

Conclusion: Aura框架作为通用增强方法，在航空安全和可靠性方面具有重要潜力，能够有效整合异质外部信息以提升时间序列预测性能。

Abstract: Time series forecasting has witnessed an increasing demand across diverse industrial applications, where accurate predictions are pivotal for informed decision-making. Beyond numerical time series data, reliable forecasting in practical scenarios requires integrating diverse exogenous factors. Such exogenous information is often multi-dimensional or even multimodal, introducing heterogeneous interactions that unimodal time series models struggle to capture. In this paper, we delve into an aviation maintenance scenario and identify three distinct types of exogenous factors that influence temporal dynamics through distinct interaction modes. Based on this empirical insight, we propose Aura, a universal framework that explicitly organizes and encodes heterogeneous external information according to its interaction mode with the target time series. Specifically, Aura utilizes a tailored tripartite encoding mechanism to embed heterogeneous features into well-established time series models, ensuring seamless integration of non-sequential context. Extensive experiments on a large-scale, three-year industrial dataset from China Southern Airlines, covering the Boeing 777 and Airbus A320 fleets, demonstrate that Aura consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance across all baselines and exhibits superior adaptability. Our findings highlight Aura's potential as a general-purpose enhancement for aviation safety and reliability.

</details>


### [78] [Axiomatic On-Manifold Shapley via Optimal Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05093)
*Cenwei Zhang,Lin Zhu,Manxi Lin,Lei You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于最优生成流的流形上Aumann-Shapley归因理论，通过Wasserstein-2测地线解决基线选择问题，实现严格流形一致性并超越现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Shapley归因方法因启发式基线选择而产生流形外伪影，现有生成方法存在几何效率低下和离散化漂移问题，需要建立严格的流形上归因理论

Method: 提出基于最优生成流的Aumann-Shapley归因形式理论，证明梯度线积分是满足效率和几何公理（特别是重参数化不变性）的唯一泛函；通过选择将先验分布传输到数据分布的动能最小化Wasserstein-2测地线来解决路径模糊性问题

Result: 建立了规范的归因族，在加性模型中恢复经典Shapley值，提供针对流近似误差的可证明稳定性边界；实验表明方法优于基线，通过零流一致性误差实现严格流形一致性，并在结构感知总变差指标上表现出优越的语义对齐

Conclusion: 通过将基线选择重构为变分问题，提出了基于最优传输流的流形上归因理论框架，解决了传统方法的流形外伪影问题，为可解释AI提供了严格的理论基础和实用方法

Abstract: Shapley-based attribution is critical for post-hoc XAI but suffers from off-manifold artifacts due to heuristic baselines. While generative methods attempt to address this, they often introduce geometric inefficiency and discretization drift. We propose a formal theory of on-manifold Aumann-Shapley attributions driven by optimal generative flows. We prove a representation theorem establishing the gradient line integral as the unique functional satisfying efficiency and geometric axioms, notably reparameterization invariance. To resolve path ambiguity, we select the kinetic-energy-minimizing Wasserstein-2 geodesic transporting a prior to the data distribution. This yields a canonical attribution family that recovers classical Shapley for additive models and admits provable stability bounds against flow approximation errors. By reframing baseline selection as a variational problem, our method experimentally outperforms baselines, achieving strict manifold adherence via vanishing Flow Consistency Error and superior semantic alignment characterized by Structure-Aware Total Variation. Our code is on https://github.com/cenweizhang/OTFlowSHAP.

</details>


### [79] [FedBCD:Communication-Efficient Accelerated Block Coordinate Gradient Descent for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05116)
*Junkang Liu,Fanhua Shang,Yuanyuan Liu,Hongying Liu,Yuangang Li,YunXiang Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedBCGD和FedBCGD+算法，通过参数块分割和选择性上传降低联邦学习中大规模模型（如Vision Transformer）的通信开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在大规模模型（如Vision Transformer）训练中每轮通信开销过高，需要降低通信复杂度

Method: 将模型参数分割为多个块（包括共享块），每个客户端只上传特定参数块；FedBCGD+增加了客户端漂移控制和随机方差缩减

Result: 理论分析显示通信复杂度比现有方法降低1/N倍（N为参数块数），收敛速度更快；实证结果优于最先进算法

Conclusion: FedBCGD和FedBCGD+是首个针对大规模深度模型的参数块通信方法，显著降低了联邦学习的通信开销并加速了收敛

Abstract: Although Federated Learning has been widely studied in recent years, there are still high overhead expenses in each communication round for large-scale models such as Vision Transformer. To lower the communication complexity, we propose a novel Federated Block Coordinate Gradient Descent (FedBCGD) method for communication efficiency. The proposed method splits model parameters into several blocks, including a shared block and enables uploading a specific parameter block by each client, which can significantly reduce communication overhead. Moreover, we also develop an accelerated FedBCGD algorithm (called FedBCGD+) with client drift control and stochastic variance reduction. To the best of our knowledge, this paper is the first work on parameter block communication for training large-scale deep models. We also provide the convergence analysis for the proposed algorithms. Our theoretical results show that the communication complexities of our algorithms are a factor $1/N$ lower than those of existing methods, where $N$ is the number of parameter blocks, and they enjoy much faster convergence than their counterparts. Empirical results indicate the superiority of the proposed algorithms compared to state-of-the-art algorithms.
  The code is available at https://github.com/junkangLiu0/FedBCGD.

</details>


### [80] [Federated Causal Discovery Across Heterogeneous Datasets under Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05149)
*Maximilian Hahn,Alina Zajak,Dominik Heider,Adèle Helena Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了fedCI，一种联邦条件独立性检验方法，用于处理跨多个隐私受限、变量集不同的异构数据集，并在此基础上开发了fedCI-IOD算法，实现联邦环境下的因果发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于数据隐私法规和跨站点异质性的限制，传统的因果发现方法需要集中式数据集，这在多数据集场景中不可行。需要一种能够处理变量集不同、站点特定效应和混合变量类型的联邦解决方案。

Method: 使用联邦迭代重加权最小二乘法（IRLS）估计广义线性模型参数，构建似然比检验进行条件独立性测试。基于此开发fedCI-IOD算法，扩展了IOD算法，采用联邦元分析策略替代原有方法。

Result: fedCI-IOD在保护隐私的同时显著提高了统计功效，性能接近完全池化分析，并缓解了局部样本量小带来的伪影。提供了Python包、R实现和Web应用等工具。

Conclusion: fedCI和fedCI-IOD为联邦环境下的条件独立性检验和因果发现提供了隐私保护、用户友好的解决方案，特别适用于存在潜在混杂因素的分布式异构数据集。

Abstract: Causal discovery across multiple datasets is often constrained by data privacy regulations and cross-site heterogeneity, limiting the use of conventional methods that require a single, centralized dataset. To address these challenges, we introduce fedCI, a federated conditional independence test that rigorously handles heterogeneous datasets with non-identical sets of variables, site-specific effects, and mixed variable types, including continuous, ordinal, binary, and categorical variables. At its core, fedCI uses a federated Iteratively Reweighted Least Squares (IRLS) procedure to estimate the parameters of generalized linear models underlying likelihood-ratio tests for conditional independence. Building on this, we develop fedCI-IOD, a federated extension of the Integration of Overlapping Datasets (IOD) algorithm, that replaces its meta-analysis strategy and enables, for the fist time, federated causal discovery under latent confounding across distributed and heterogeneous datasets. By aggregating evidence federatively, fedCI-IOD not only preserves privacy but also substantially enhances statistical power, achieving performance comparable to fully pooled analyses and mitigating artifacts from low local sample sizes. Our tools are publicly available as the fedCI Python package, a privacy-preserving R implementation of IOD, and a web application for the fedCI-IOD pipeline, providing versatile, user-friendly solutions for federated conditional independence testing and causal discovery.

</details>


### [81] [Balancing Privacy-Quality-Efficiency in Federated Learning through Round-Based Interleaving of Protection Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05158)
*Yenan Wang,Carla Fabiana Chiasserini,Elad Michael Schiller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Alt-FL：结合差分隐私、同态加密和合成数据的联邦学习隐私保护框架，通过轮次交错策略实现隐私保护、学习质量和效率的平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中隐私保护、学习质量和效率之间的平衡是一个挑战。差分隐私会降低学习质量，同态加密则带来显著系统开销。需要一种能灵活权衡这些因素的新方法。

Method: 提出Alt-FL框架，结合差分隐私、同态加密和合成数据，采用轮次交错策略。引入三种新方法：隐私交错(PI)、带DP的合成交错(SI/DP)、带HE的合成交错(SI/HE)。使用LeNet-5模型在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST上系统评估对抗重构攻击的能力。

Result: 在高隐私保护级别下，PI方法实现最平衡的权衡；在中等隐私要求下，基于DP的方法更优。引入新的攻击者中心框架，可在不同交错方法间公平比较攻击成功率。

Conclusion: Alt-FL为不同隐私和资源约束下的隐私保护联邦学习方法选择提供了基础。该框架通过灵活的交错策略实现了隐私保护、学习质量和效率的有效权衡。

Abstract: In federated learning (FL), balancing privacy protection, learning quality, and efficiency remains a challenge. Privacy protection mechanisms, such as Differential Privacy (DP), degrade learning quality, or, as in the case of Homomorphic Encryption (HE), incur substantial system overhead. To address this, we propose Alt-FL, a privacy-preserving FL framework that combines DP, HE, and synthetic data via a novel round-based interleaving strategy. Alt-FL introduces three new methods, Privacy Interleaving (PI), Synthetic Interleaving with DP (SI/DP), and Synthetic Interleaving with HE (SI/HE), that enable flexible quality-efficiency trade-offs while providing privacy protection.
  We systematically evaluate Alt-FL against representative reconstruction attacks, including Deep Leakage from Gradients, Inverting Gradients, When the Curious Abandon Honesty, and Robbing the Fed, using a LeNet-5 model on CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST. To enable fair comparison between DP- and HE-based defenses, we introduce a new attacker-centric framework that compares empirical attack success rates across the three proposed interleaving methods. Our results show that, for the studied attacker model and dataset, PI achieves the most balanced trade-offs at high privacy protection levels, while DP-based methods are preferable at intermediate privacy requirements. We also discuss how such results can be the basis for selecting privacy-preserving FL methods under varying privacy and resource constraints.

</details>


### [82] [Incentive Aware AI Regulations: A Credal Characterisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05175)
*Anurag Singh,Julian Rodemann,Rajeev Verma,Siu Lun Chau,Krikamol Muandet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将AI监管建模为不确定条件下的机制设计问题，提出监管机制框架，通过许可证映射实证证据到市场份额，迫使提供商对其模型满足监管要求的能力下注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险的机器学习应用需要严格监管，但策略性的ML提供商经常规避监管以降低开发成本。需要设计可执行的AI监管框架，确保合规提供商参与市场，同时让不合规提供商自我排除。

Method: 将AI监管建模为机制设计问题，引入监管机制框架：将模型实证证据映射到市场份额许可证。提供商可以从一组许可证中选择，迫使其对模型满足监管要求的能力下注。研究实现完美市场结果的机制，即(a)驱动不合规提供商自我排除，(b)确保合规提供商参与。

Result: 证明机制具有完美市场结果当且仅当不合规分布集合构成一个信度集（封闭、凸的概率测度集合）。这一结果通过建立监管机制与不合规分布集合之间的对偶性，连接了机制设计和不精确概率理论。实验展示了在监管预测中虚假特征使用和公平性方面的实际应用。

Conclusion: 该框架在机制设计和不精确概率的交叉领域提供了新见解，为开发可执行的AI监管奠定了基础。通过将监管问题形式化为机制设计，能够确保合规提供商参与市场，同时有效排除不合规提供商。

Abstract: While high-stakes ML applications demand strict regulations, strategic ML providers often evade them to lower development costs. To address this challenge, we cast AI regulation as a mechanism design problem under uncertainty and introduce regulation mechanisms: a framework that maps empirical evidence from models to a license for some market share. The providers can select from a set of licenses, effectively forcing them to bet on their model's ability to fulfil regulation. We aim at regulation mechanisms that achieve perfect market outcome, i.e. (a) drive non-compliant providers to self-exclude, and (b) ensure participation from compliant providers. We prove that a mechanism has perfect market outcome if and only if the set of non-compliant distributions forms a credal set, i.e., a closed, convex set of probability measures. This result connects mechanism design and imprecise probability by establishing a duality between regulation mechanisms and the set of non-compliant distributions. We also demonstrate these mechanisms in practice via experiments on regulating use of spurious features for prediction and fairness. Our framework provides new insights at the intersection of mechanism design and imprecise probability, offering a foundation for development of enforceable AI regulations.

</details>


### [83] [Towards a data-scale independent regulariser for robust sparse identification of non-linear dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05201)
*Jay Raut,Daniel N. Wilke,Stephan Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STCV算法解决数据归一化对稀疏回归方法（特别是SINDy）的负面影响，通过基于统计显著性的阈值处理替代传统的幅度阈值，使模型发现过程对数据缩放具有不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据归一化作为常见的预处理步骤，会严重扭曲基于幅度的稀疏回归方法（如SINDy）对控制方程的发现。归一化与测量噪声的相互作用破坏了稀疏性核心假设，导致发现的模型变得稠密、不可解释且物理不正确。

Method: 提出序列化变异系数阈值法（STCV），一种计算高效的稀疏回归算法。该方法使用无量纲统计指标——系数存在性（CP）替代传统的幅度阈值，CP评估模型库中候选项的统计有效性和一致性。这种从幅度到统计显著性的转变使发现过程对任意数据缩放具有不变性。

Result: 在规范动力系统和实际工程问题（包括物理质量-弹簧-阻尼器实验）上的综合基准测试表明，STCV在归一化、噪声数据集上始终显著优于标准STLSQ和E-SINDy。STCV方法即使在其他方法失败时也能成功识别正确的稀疏物理定律。

Conclusion: STCV通过减轻归一化的扭曲效应，使稀疏系统识别成为更可靠和自动化的现实应用工具，从而增强了模型的可解释性和可信度。

Abstract: Data normalisation, a common and often necessary preprocessing step in engineering and scientific applications, can severely distort the discovery of governing equations by magnitudebased sparse regression methods. This issue is particularly acute for the Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) framework, where the core assumption of sparsity is undermined by the interaction between data scaling and measurement noise. The resulting discovered models can be dense, uninterpretable, and physically incorrect. To address this critical vulnerability, we introduce the Sequential Thresholding of Coefficient of Variation (STCV), a novel, computationally efficient sparse regression algorithm that is inherently robust to data scaling. STCV replaces conventional magnitude-based thresholding with a dimensionless statistical metric, the Coefficient Presence (CP), which assesses the statistical validity and consistency of candidate terms in the model library. This shift from magnitude to statistical significance makes the discovery process invariant to arbitrary data scaling. Through comprehensive benchmarking on canonical dynamical systems and practical engineering problems, including a physical mass-spring-damper experiment, we demonstrate that STCV consistently and significantly outperforms standard Sequential Thresholding Least Squares (STLSQ) and Ensemble-SINDy (E-SINDy) on normalised, noisy datasets. The results show that STCV-based methods can successfully identify the correct, sparse physical laws even when other methods fail. By mitigating the distorting effects of normalisation, STCV makes sparse system identification a more reliable and automated tool for real-world applications, thereby enhancing model interpretability and trustworthiness.

</details>


### [84] [Stable-LoRA: Stabilizing Feature Learning of Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05204)
*Yize Wu,Ke Gao,Ling Li,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Stable-LoRA，通过权重收缩优化策略解决LoRA特征学习不稳定性问题，在保持非零初始化优势的同时提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LoRA在实践中效果显著，但其理论基础尤其是特征学习稳定性方面研究不足。研究发现LoRA在适当超参数和初始化下可实现自稳定特征学习，但必要的非零初始化会破坏这种自稳定性，导致性能下降

Method: 提出Stable-LoRA方法，采用权重收缩优化策略，在训练早期逐步收缩A矩阵，动态增强LoRA特征学习的稳定性，同时保留非零初始化的优势

Result: 实验表明Stable-LoRA在各种模型和任务上一致优于其他基线方法，无需额外内存开销，计算开销可忽略不计

Conclusion: Stable-LoRA有效解决了LoRA特征学习的不稳定性问题，在理论和实证上都得到验证，为参数高效微调提供了更稳定的解决方案

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a widely adopted parameter-efficient method for fine-tuning Large Langauge Models. It updates the weight matrix as $W=W_0+sBA$, where $W_0$ is the original frozen weight, $s$ is a scaling factor and $A$,$B$ are trainable low-rank matrices. Despite its robust empirical effectiveness, the theoretical foundations of LoRA remain insufficiently understood, particularly with respect to feature learning stability. In this paper, we first establish that, LoRA can, in principle, naturally achieve and sustain stable feature learning (i.e., be self-stabilized) under appropriate hyper-parameters and initializations of $A$ and $B$. However, we also uncover a fundamental limitation that the necessary non-zero initialization of $A$ compromises self-stability, leading to suboptimal performances. To address this challenge, we propose Stable-LoRA, a weight-shrinkage optimization strategy that dynamically enhances stability of LoRA feature learning. By progressively shrinking $A$ during the earliest training steps, Stable-LoRA is both theoretically and empirically validated to effectively eliminate instability of LoRA feature learning while preserving the benefits of the non-zero start. Experiments show that Stable-LoRA consistently outperforms other baselines across diverse models and tasks, with no additional memory usage and only negligible computation overheads. The code is available at https://github.com/Yize-Wu/Stable-LoRA.

</details>


### [85] [Early Warning of Intraoperative Adverse Events via Transformer-Driven Multi-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05212)
*Xueyao Wang,Xiuding Cai,Honglin Shang,Yaoyao Zhu,Yu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了IAENet框架，用于术中不良事件的早期预警，通过多标签学习、时间感知特征融合和标签约束重加权损失来解决现有方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中不良事件早期预警对降低手术风险和改善患者安全至关重要。现有深度学习方法存在三个主要挑战：忽视不良事件间的依赖关系、未充分利用异质临床数据、以及医疗数据集中固有的类别不平衡问题。

Method: 1. 构建首个术中多标签不良事件数据集(MuAE)，涵盖六种关键事件；2. 提出基于Transformer的多标签学习框架(IAENet)，包含改进的时间感知特征调制模块(TAFiLM)用于静态协变量和动态变量的鲁棒融合及复杂时间依赖建模；3. 引入带共现正则化的标签约束重加权损失(LCRLoss)来缓解事件内不平衡并增强频繁共现事件间的结构化一致性。

Result: IAENet在5、10和15分钟早期预警任务上持续优于强基线方法，平均F1分数分别提升+5.05%、+2.82%和+7.57%。这些结果证明了IAENet在支持临床实践中智能术中决策的潜力。

Conclusion: IAENet通过有效融合异质临床数据、建模事件间依赖关系并解决类别不平衡问题，为术中不良事件的早期预警提供了有效的解决方案，具有临床应用的潜力。

Abstract: Early warning of intraoperative adverse events plays a vital role in reducing surgical risk and improving patient safety. While deep learning has shown promise in predicting the single adverse event, several key challenges remain: overlooking adverse event dependencies, underutilizing heterogeneous clinical data, and suffering from the class imbalance inherent in medical datasets. To address these issues, we construct the first Multi-label Adverse Events dataset (MuAE) for intraoperative adverse events prediction, covering six critical events. Next, we propose a novel Transformerbased multi-label learning framework (IAENet) that combines an improved Time-Aware Feature-wise Linear Modulation (TAFiLM) module for static covariates and dynamic variables robust fusion and complex temporal dependencies modeling. Furthermore, we introduce a Label-Constrained Reweighting Loss (LCRLoss) with co-occurrence regularization to effectively mitigate intra-event imbalance and enforce structured consistency among frequently co-occurring events. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IAENet consistently outperforms strong baselines on 5, 10, and 15-minute early warning tasks, achieving improvements of +5.05%, +2.82%, and +7.57% on average F1 score. These results highlight the potential of IAENet for supporting intelligent intraoperative decision-making in clinical practice.

</details>


### [86] [The Geometric Inductive Bias of Grokking: Bypassing Phase Transitions via Architectural Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05228)
*Alper Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过干预性架构修改研究Transformer中的grokking现象，发现限制表示幅度和消除数据依赖注意力路由可以显著加速或消除延迟泛化，但效果依赖于任务对称性与架构先验的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机制可解释性方法主要依赖训练后分析，本研究采用干预性方法：通过修改架构拓扑来先验测试假设，研究Transformer在循环模加法任务中出现的grokking（延迟泛化）现象，探究特定架构自由度是否会延长记忆阶段。

Method: 采用两种架构干预：1）完全有界的球形拓扑，在整个残差流中强制L2归一化，并使用固定温度尺度的解嵌入矩阵，消除基于幅度的自由度；2）均匀注意力消融，用均匀分布覆盖数据依赖的查询-键路由，将注意力层简化为连续词袋聚合器。使用非交换S5置换组合作为负对照来验证效果特异性。

Result: 球形拓扑在没有权重衰减的情况下将grokking起始时间减少超过20倍；均匀注意力消融模型在所有种子下实现100%泛化，完全绕过grokking延迟。但在S5负对照任务中，球形约束并未加速泛化，表明消除记忆阶段强烈依赖于架构先验与任务内在对称性的对齐。

Conclusion: 架构自由度显著影响grokking现象，提供干预性证据支持结构性视角预测训练动态。消除记忆阶段需要架构先验与任务对称性的对齐，而非通用的优化稳定器。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability typically relies on post-hoc analysis of trained networks. We instead adopt an interventional approach: testing hypotheses a priori by modifying architectural topology to observe training dynamics. We study grokking - delayed generalization in Transformers trained on cyclic modular addition (Zp) - investigating if specific architectural degrees of freedom prolong the memorization phase.
  We identify two independent structural factors in standard Transformers: unbounded representational magnitude and data-dependent attention routing. First, we introduce a fully bounded spherical topology enforcing L2 normalization throughout the residual stream and an unembedding matrix with a fixed temperature scale. This removes magnitude-based degrees of freedom, reducing grokking onset time by over 20x without weight decay. Second, a Uniform Attention Ablation overrides data-dependent query-key routing with a uniform distribution, reducing the attention layer to a Continuous Bag-of-Words (CBOW) aggregator. Despite removing adaptive routing, these models achieve 100% generalization across all seeds and bypass the grokking delay entirely.
  To evaluate whether this acceleration is a task-specific geometric alignment rather than a generic optimization stabilizer, we use non-commutative S5 permutation composition as a negative control. Enforcing spherical constraints on S5 does not accelerate generalization. This suggests eliminating the memorization phase depends strongly on aligning architectural priors with the task's intrinsic symmetries. Together, these findings provide interventional evidence that architectural degrees of freedom substantially influence grokking, suggesting a predictive structural perspective on training dynamics.

</details>


### [87] [SlideSparse: Fast and Flexible (2N-2):2N Structured Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05232)
*Hanyong Shao,Yingbo Hao,Ting Song,Yan Xia,Di Zhang,Shaohan Huang,Xun Wu,Songchen Xu,Le Xu,Li Dong,Zewen Chi,Yi Zou,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SlideSparse系统首次在商用GPU上为(2N-2):2N稀疏模式（如6:8）提供稀疏张量核心加速，通过滑动窗口分解和激活提升技术，在保持精度的同时实现接近理论上限的1.33倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA的2:4稀疏张量核心虽然提供2倍吞吐量，但要求严格的50%剪枝，这会严重损害LLM推理精度（如Qwen3从54%降至15%）。更温和的(2N-2):2N模式（如6:8，25%剪枝）能保持精度，但缺乏硬件支持，只能回退到密集执行而无法利用稀疏性优势。

Method: SlideSparse采用滑动窗口分解技术，将任意(2N-2):2N权重块重构为N-1个重叠的2:4兼容窗口，无精度损失；激活提升技术将相应的激活重排融合到每令牌量化中，成本极低。系统集成到vLLM框架中。

Result: 在计算受限工作负载下，Qwen2.5-7B模型在6:8权重稀疏度下测得1.33倍加速比，接近理论上限N/(N-1)=4/3。系统支持多种GPU（A100、H100、B200、RTX 4090/5080、DGX-spark）、精度（FP4、INT8、FP8、BF16、FP16）和模型家族（Llama、Qwen、BitNet）。

Conclusion: SlideSparse首次实现了(2N-2):2N稀疏模式的硬件加速，建立了保持精度的LLM加速实用路径，代码已开源。

Abstract: NVIDIA's 2:4 Sparse Tensor Cores deliver 2x throughput but demand strict 50% pruning -- a ratio that collapses LLM reasoning accuracy (Qwen3: 54% to 15%). Milder $(2N-2):2N$ patterns (e.g., 6:8, 25% pruning) preserve accuracy yet receive no hardware support, falling back to dense execution without any benefit from sparsity. We present SlideSparse, the first system to unlock Sparse Tensor Core acceleration for the $(2N-2):2N$ model family on commodity GPUs. Our Sliding Window Decomposition reconstructs any $(2N-2):2N$ weight block into $N-1$ overlapping 2:4-compliant windows without any accuracy loss; Activation Lifting fuses the corresponding activation rearrangement into per-token quantization at near-zero cost. Integrated into vLLM, SlideSparse is evaluated across various GPUs (A100, H100, B200, RTX 4090, RTX 5080, DGX-spark), precisions (FP4, INT8, FP8, BF16, FP16), and model families (Llama, Qwen, BitNet). On compute-bound workloads, the measured speedup ratio (1.33x) approaches the theoretical upper-bound $N/(N-1)=4/3$ at 6:8 weight sparsity in Qwen2.5-7B, establishing $(2N-2):2N$ as a practical path to accuracy-preserving LLM acceleration. Code available at https://github.com/bcacdwk/vllmbench.

</details>


### [88] [Recursive Inference Machines for Neural Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05234)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出递归推理机(RIMs)，将神经推理与经典推理引擎的递归推理机制相结合，改进现有神经推理模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经推理器（如TRMs）结合神经主干与专门推理方案，而经典随机推理系统使用推理规则处理随机模型。本文旨在桥接这两种范式，将经典推理引擎的递归推理机制显式融入神经推理框架

Method: 提出递归推理机(RIMs)框架，显式整合受经典推理引擎启发的递归推理机制。将TRMs表达为RIMs实例，并通过重加权组件扩展，提升推理能力

Result: 在ARC-AGI-1、ARC-AGI-2和Sudoku Extreme等挑战性推理基准上获得更好性能。在表格数据分类任务上超越TabPFNs，展示跨任务推理改进能力

Conclusion: RIMs成功桥接了神经推理与经典推理范式，通过显式整合递归推理机制提升推理性能，为复杂问题求解提供更强大的神经推理框架

Abstract: Neural reasoners such as Tiny Recursive Models (TRMs) solve complex problems by combining neural backbones with specialized inference schemes. Such inference schemes have been a central component of stochastic reasoning systems, where inference rules are applied to a stochastic model to derive answers to complex queries. In this work, we bridge these two paradigms by introducing Recursive Inference Machines (RIMs), a neural reasoning framework that explicitly incorporates recursive inference mechanisms inspired by classical inference engines. We show that TRMs can be expressed as an instance of RIMs, allowing us to extend them through a reweighting component, yielding better performance on challenging reasoning benchmarks, including ARC-AGI-1, ARC-AGI-2, and Sudoku Extreme. Furthermore, we show that RIMs can be used to improve reasoning on other tasks, such as the classification of tabular data, outperforming TabPFNs.

</details>


### [89] [A Behaviour-Aware Federated Forecasting Framework for Distributed Stand-Alone Wind Turbines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05263)
*Bowen Li,Xiufeng Liu,Maria Sinziiana Astefanoaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段联邦学习框架：首先基于长期行为统计使用DRS初始化和递归自动分割聚类风机，然后通过FedAvg训练聚类特定的LSTM模型，用于短期风电功率预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的短期风电功率预测对电网调度和市场运营至关重要，但集中化风机数据会引发隐私、成本和异质性担忧。现有方法难以处理分布式风机数据集的隐私保护和异质性问题。

Method: 1. 第一阶段：使用双轮盘选择(DRS)初始化和递归自动分割细化，基于长期行为统计对风机进行聚类；2. 第二阶段：通过联邦平均(FedAvg)算法为每个聚类训练特定的LSTM预测模型

Result: 在丹麦400台独立风机上的实验表明：DRS-auto方法能够发现行为一致的聚类组；在保持数据本地化的同时实现了有竞争力的预测精度；行为感知分组持续优于地理分区方法，与强基准k-means++性能相当

Conclusion: 该框架为异构分布式风机群提供了一种实用的隐私友好解决方案，通过行为感知聚类和联邦学习平衡了预测精度与隐私保护需求

Abstract: Accurate short-term wind power forecasting is essential for grid dispatch and market operations, yet centralising turbine data raises privacy, cost, and heterogeneity concerns. We propose a two-stage federated learning framework that first clusters turbines by long-term behavioural statistics using Double Roulette Selection (DRS) initialisation with recursive Auto-split refinement, and then trains cluster-specific LSTM models via FedAvg. Experiments on 400 stand-alone turbines in Denmark show that DRS-auto discovers behaviourally coherent groups and achieves competitive forecasting accuracy while preserving data locality. Behaviour-aware grouping consistently outperforms geographic partitioning and matches strong k-means++ baselines, suggesting a practical privacy-friendly solution for heterogeneous distributed turbine fleets.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Word Error Rate: Auditing the Diversity Tax in Speech Recognition through Dataset Cartography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05267)
*Ting-Hui Cheng,Line H. Clemmensen,Sneha Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了ASR系统中WER指标的局限性，提出了SDI指标来量化模型失败的内在因素，并展示了语义指标能揭示WER忽略的系统性偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR评估主要依赖词错误率(WER)，但这种基于词符计数的指标无法捕捉语义保真度，且会掩盖"多样性税"问题——边缘化和非典型说话者因系统性识别失败而承受的不成比例的负担。

Method: 1. 系统评估更广泛的非线性和语义指标；2. 引入样本难度指数(SDI)来量化内在人口统计和声学因素如何驱动模型失败；3. 通过数据制图将SDI可视化；4. 使用EmbER和SemDist等语义指标暴露隐藏的系统性偏见。

Result: 研究发现EmbER和SemDist等语义指标能够揭示WER忽略的隐藏系统性偏见和模型间分歧。SDI能够有效量化模型失败的内在驱动因素，为模型审计提供了新工具。

Conclusion: 该研究是构建稳健审计框架的第一步，使开发者能够在部署前审计和缓解ASR差异，实现前瞻性安全分析。语义指标和SDI为更全面的ASR评估提供了新方向。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems are predominantly evaluated using the Word Error Rate (WER). However, raw token-level metrics fail to capture semantic fidelity and routinely obscures the `diversity tax', the disproportionate burden on marginalized and atypical speaker due to systematic recognition failures. In this paper, we explore the limitations of relying solely on lexical counts by systematically evaluating a broader class of non-linear and semantic metrics. To enable rigorous model auditing, we introduce the sample difficulty index (SDI), a novel metric that quantifies how intrinsic demographic and acoustic factors drive model failure. By mapping SDI on data cartography, we demonstrate that metrics EmbER and SemDist expose hidden systemic biases and inter-model disagreements that WER ignores. Finally, our findings are the first steps towards a robust audit framework for prospective safety analysis, empowering developers to audit and mitigate ASR disparities prior to deployment.

</details>


### [91] [Whispering to a Blackbox: Bootstrapping Frozen OCR with Visual Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05276)
*Samandar Samandarov,Nazirjon Ismoiljonov,Abdullah Sattorov,Temirlan Sabyrbayev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Whisperer框架通过扩散模型预处理输入图像，以行为克隆方式增强冻结预训练模型性能，在文本识别任务上显著降低字符错误率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结预训练模型虽然稳定高效，但在特定任务上因数据分布不匹配而表现不佳。需要一种不修改模型权重就能提升性能的方法

Method: 提出Whisperer视觉提示框架，使用扩散模型作为预处理器，通过四阶段训练课程学习：随机采样扩散中间输出，选择偶然改善CER的结果，然后用行为克隆训练模型复制这些改进

Result: 在30万张退化合成文本图像数据集上，实现了8%绝对（10.6%相对）的字符错误率降低，超越了CLAHE等手工设计基线方法

Conclusion: 通过向冻结OCR模型"低语"输入增强，可以在不修改模型权重的情况下改善不完美分类器的性能，该方法样本效率高且避免了传统强化学习的缺陷

Abstract: In the landscape of modern machine learning, frozen pre-trained models provide stability and efficiency but often underperform on specific tasks due to mismatched data distributions. This paper introduces the Whisperer, a novel visual prompting framework that learns diffusion-based preprocessors to adapt inputs in pixel space, effectively "whispering" enhancements to frozen downstream models like EasyOCR. By framing the process as behavioral cloning of stochastically discovered improvement policies, our method achieves an 8% absolute (10.6% relative) reduction in Character Error Rate (CER) on a challenging dataset of 300k degraded synthetic text images, surpassing hand-engineered baselines such as CLAHE. The key innovation is a four-stage training curriculum that uses behavioral cloning to amplify "lucky" improvements discovered through the stochastic exploration of a partially trained diffusion model. This approach is highly sample-efficient and avoids the pitfalls of traditional reinforcement learning. Crucially, we frame this not as naive reinforcement learning, but as behavioral cloning of an exploration policy: we stochastically sample intermediate diffusion outputs, select those that improve CER by chance, and then train the model to reproduce them. This bootstrapping curriculum (4 stages over 60 GPU-hours) amplifies random successes into a systematic strategy. In summary, by whispering to the frozen OCR through its inputs, we improve an imperfect classifier without touching its weights.

</details>


### [92] [Knowledge Divergence and the Value of Debate for Scalable Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05293)
*Robin Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文建立了辩论与RLAIF之间的首个形式化联系，通过知识分歧的几何参数化证明了辩论优势的闭式解，并识别了三种知识分歧机制及其对辩论有效性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全领域提出了辩论和基于AI反馈的强化学习（RLAIF）两种可扩展监督方法，但缺乏形式化框架来关联这两种方法或描述辩论何时具有优势。需要建立理论连接并量化辩论的价值。

Method: 通过模型表示子空间之间的主角度参数化知识分歧的几何结构，推导辩论优势的精确闭式解。分析三种知识分歧机制（共享、单边、组合），并提供存在性结果和负面结果。

Result: 当模型共享相同训练语料时，辩论退化为类似RLAIF的单智能体方法；当知识分歧时，辩论优势呈现从二次机制（辩论益处可忽略）到线性机制（辩论至关重要）的相变。证明了辩论可以实现任一模型单独无法达到的结果，但也发现足够强的对抗性激励会导致组合机制中的协调失败。

Conclusion: 该研究首次建立了辩论与RLAIF之间的形式化连接，为理解何时需要对抗性监督协议提供了几何基础，并与跨模型潜在知识激发问题相关联，揭示了知识分歧几何结构如何决定辩论的有效性。

Abstract: AI safety via debate and reinforcement learning from AI feedback (RLAIF) are both proposed methods for scalable oversight of advanced AI systems, yet no formal framework relates them or characterizes when debate offers an advantage. We analyze this by parameterizing debate's value through the geometry of knowledge divergence between debating models. Using principal angles between models' representation subspaces, we prove that the debate advantage admits an exact closed form. When models share identical training corpora, debate reduces to RLAIF-like where a single-agent method recovers the same optimum. When models possess divergent knowledge, debate advantage scales with a phase transition from quadratic regime (debate offers negligible benefit) to linear regime (debate is essential). We classify three regimes of knowledge divergence (shared, one-sided, and compositional) and provide existence results showing that debate can achieve outcomes inaccessible to either model alone, alongside a negative result showing that sufficiently strong adversarial incentives cause coordination failure in the compositional regime, with a sharp threshold separating effective from ineffective debate. We offer the first formal connection between debate and RLAIF, a geometric foundation for understanding when adversarial oversight protocols are justified, and connection to the problem of eliciting latent knowledge across models with complementary information.

</details>


### [93] [WavSLM: Single-Stream Speech Language Modeling via WavLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05299)
*Luca Della Libera,Cem Subakan,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WavSLM：首个基于单流自回归训练的语音语言模型，通过量化WavLM表示实现语义和声学信息的联合建模，无需文本监督或文本预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音语言模型大多依赖文本监督、分层token流或复杂混合架构，偏离了在文本领域证明有效的单流生成预训练范式。需要探索简单自回归训练在语音领域的扩展。

Method: 将自监督WavLM表示量化和蒸馏为单一码本，优化自回归下一块预测目标，在单token流中联合建模语义和声学信息，无需文本监督。

Result: 在一致性基准和语音生成任务上取得竞争性性能，同时使用更少参数、更少训练数据，并支持流式推理。

Conclusion: WavSLM证明了简单自回归训练范式可扩展至语音领域，实现语义和声学信息的联合建模，为语音语言模型提供了更简洁有效的架构。

Abstract: Large language models show that simple autoregressive training can yield scalable and coherent generation, but extending this paradigm to speech remains challenging due to the entanglement of semantic and acoustic information. Most existing speech language models rely on text supervision, hierarchical token streams, or complex hybrid architectures, departing from the single-stream generative pretraining paradigm that has proven effective in text. In this work, we introduce WavSLM, a speech language model trained by quantizing and distilling self-supervised WavLM representations into a single codebook and optimizing an autoregressive next-chunk prediction objective. WavSLM jointly models semantic and acoustic information within a single token stream without text supervision or text pretraining. Despite its simplicity, it achieves competitive performance on consistency benchmarks and speech generation while using fewer parameters, less training data, and supporting streaming inference. Demo samples are available at https://lucadellalib.github.io/wavslm-web/.

</details>


### [94] [GALACTIC: Global and Local Agnostic Counterfactuals for Time-series Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05318)
*Christos Fragkathoulas,Eleni Psaroudaki,Themis Palpanas,Evaggelia Pitoura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GALACTIC是首个统一框架，通过反事实解释为无监督时间序列聚类提供局部和全局可解释性，使用聚类感知优化和最小描述长度目标生成稀疏扰动和简洁的全局摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列聚类的可解释性方法（基于特征归因或元数据）无法识别使实例跨越聚类边界的转变。虽然反事实解释能识别改变模型预测所需的最小时间扰动，但主要局限于监督设置。需要为无监督时间序列聚类开发统一的局部和全局反事实解释框架。

Method: GALACTIC框架包含两个层面：1) 局部层面：通过聚类感知优化目标生成扰动，尊重目标和底层聚类分配；2) 全局层面：制定代表性反事实解释选择问题，提出最小描述长度目标提取非冗余的全局解释摘要，表征聚类间转变。证明MDL目标是超模的，可转化为单调子模集函数，从而设计高效的贪心选择算法，具有(1-1/e)近似保证。

Result: 在UCR Archive上的广泛实验评估表明，GALACTIC相比为问题适配的最先进基线方法，能生成显著更稀疏的局部反事实解释和更简洁的全局摘要，首次提供了通过反事实解释聚类时间序列的统一方法。

Conclusion: GALACTIC是首个为无监督时间序列聚类提供统一局部和全局反事实解释的框架，通过聚类感知优化和最小描述长度目标实现了稀疏扰动和简洁的全局摘要，具有理论保证和实际有效性。

Abstract: Time-series clustering is a fundamental tool for pattern discovery, yet existing explainability methods, primarily based on feature attribution or metadata, fail to identify the transitions that move an instance across cluster boundaries. While Counterfactual Explanations (CEs) identify the minimal temporal perturbations required to alter the prediction of a model, they have been mostly confined to supervised settings. This paper introduces GALACTIC, the first unified framework to bridge local and global counterfactual explainability for unsupervised time-series clustering. At instance level (local), GALACTIC generates perturbations via a cluster-aware optimization objective that respects the target and underlying cluster assignments. At cluster level (global), to mitigate cognitive load and enhance interpretability, we formulate a representative CE selection problem. We propose a Minimum Description Length (MDL) objective to extract a non-redundant summary of global explanations that characterize the transitions between clusters. We prove that our MDL objective is supermodular, which allows the corresponding MDL reduction to be framed as a monotone submodular set function. This enables an efficient greedy selection algorithm with provable $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantees. Extensive experimental evaluation on the UCR Archive demonstrates that GALACTIC produces significantly sparser local CEs and more concise global summaries than state-of-the-art baselines adapted for our problem, offering the first unified approach for interpreting clustered time-series through counterfactuals.

</details>


### [95] [FairFinGAN: Fairness-aware Synthetic Financial Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05327)
*Tai Le Quy,Dung Nguyen Tuan,Trung Nguyen Thanh,Duy Tran Cong,Huyen Giang Thi Thu,Frank Hopfgartner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairFinGAN：基于WGAN的金融数据生成框架，通过分类器引入公平性约束，在生成合成金融数据的同时减轻受保护属性的偏见


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融数据集常存在偏见，可能导致自动化系统做出不公平决策。需要开发能够生成公平合成金融数据的方法，以支持无偏见的决策系统

Method: 提出FairFinGAN框架，基于Wasserstein GAN（WGAN），通过分类器将公平性约束直接整合到训练过程中，确保生成的合成数据既公平又保持下游预测任务的实用性

Result: 在五个真实金融数据集上评估，与现有基于GAN的数据生成方法比较。实验结果显示，该方法在公平性指标上表现优异，且数据实用性没有显著损失

Conclusion: FairFinGAN展示了作为金融应用中偏见感知数据生成工具的潜力，能够在保持数据实用性的同时实现更好的公平性

Abstract: Financial datasets often suffer from bias that can lead to unfair decision-making in automated systems. In this work, we propose FairFinGAN, a WGAN-based framework designed to generate synthetic financial data while mitigating bias with respect to the protected attribute. Our approach incorporates fairness constraints directly into the training process through a classifier, ensuring that the synthetic data is both fair and preserves utility for downstream predictive tasks. We evaluate our proposed model on five real-world financial datasets and compare it with existing GAN-based data generation methods. Experimental results show that our approach achieves superior fairness metrics without significant loss in data utility, demonstrating its potential as a tool for bias-aware data generation in financial applications.

</details>


### [96] [InfoFlow KV: Information-Flow-Aware KV Recomputation for Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05353)
*Xin Teng,Canyu Zhang,Shaoyi Zheng,Danyang Zhuo,Tianyi Zhou,Shengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于信息流的KV缓存选择性重计算方法，通过注意力范数信号识别关键令牌，优化长上下文问答中的检索增强生成效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文问答中的检索增强生成（RAG）受限于对大型检索上下文进行推理时预填充的瓶颈。现有方法依赖启发式或表示差异，未能建模所选令牌是否能有效影响生成过程

Method: 将选择性KV重计算建模为信息流问题，使用查询的注意力范数信号识别语义相关且结构位置合适的令牌；在推理一致的RoPE几何下计算，重建检索块的位置分配并引入信息流引导的块重排序策略

Result: 在LLM和VLM基准测试中，在可比效率预算下相比先前方法获得一致性能提升

Conclusion: 基于信息流的KV缓存选择性重计算方法能有效识别影响生成的关键令牌，显著提升长上下文问答中检索增强生成的效率

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for long-context question answering is bottlenecked by inference-time prefilling over large retrieved contexts. A common strategy is to precompute key-value (KV) caches for individual documents and selectively recompute a small subset of tokens to restore global causal dependencies, but existing methods rely on heuristics or representation discrepancies without modeling whether selected tokens can effectively influence generation. We cast selective KV recomputation as an information flow problem and show that a simple attention-norm signal from the query reliably identifies tokens that are both semantically relevant and structurally positioned to propagate information, when computed under an inference-consistent RoPE geometry. We therefore reconstruct global positional assignments for retrieved chunks and introduce an information-flow-guided chunk reordering strategy. Experiments on LLM and VLM benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains over prior methods under comparable efficiency budgets.

</details>


### [97] [Embedded Inter-Subject Variability in Adversarial Learning for Inertial Sensor-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05371)
*Francisco M. Calatrava-Nicolás,Shoko Miyauchi,Vitor Fortes Rey,Paul Lukowicz,Todor Stoyanov,Oscar Martinez Mozos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的深度对抗框架，通过整合主体间变异性概念来增强人类活动识别的泛化能力，在LOSO验证中优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人类活动识别中由于主体间变异性导致的模型泛化问题。不同个体执行相同活动的方式存在差异，这使得模型难以泛化到未见过的个体。

Method: 提出新颖的深度对抗框架，将主体间变异性概念整合到对抗任务中，鼓励学习主体不变的特征表示，从而提升HAR分类性能。

Result: 在三个成熟的HAR数据集上使用留一主体交叉验证，性能优于先前方法。对抗任务有效减少了特征空间中不同用户的主体间变异性，且优于先前工作中的对抗任务。

Conclusion: 所提出的对抗框架通过整合主体间变异性概念，能够学习主体不变的特征表示，显著提升HAR模型对新个体的泛化能力。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using data from wearable inertial sensors. An important challenge in HAR is the model's generalization capabilities to new unseen individuals due to inter-subject variability, i.e., the same activity is performed differently by different individuals. To address this problem, we propose a novel deep adversarial framework that integrates the concept of inter-subject variability in the adversarial task, thereby encouraging subject-invariant feature representations and enhancing the classification performance in the HAR problem. Our approach outperforms previous methods in three well-established HAR datasets using a leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation. Further results indicate that our proposed adversarial task effectively reduces inter-subject variability among different users in the feature space, and it outperforms adversarial tasks from previous works when integrated into our framework. Code: https://github.com/FranciscoCalatrava/EmbeddedSubjectVariability.git

</details>


### [98] [On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05433)
*Hejian Sang,Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Ran He,Zhipeng Wang,Jiachen Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OPSDC是一种通过自我蒸馏压缩推理过程的方法，仅需"简洁推理"指令即可实现自动压缩，在保持难问题思考深度的同时大幅减少简单问题的推理步骤，显著提升准确率并减少57-59%的token使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理模型在思考过程中产生大量噪声和冗余内容，这些不必要的token不仅浪费计算资源，还可能累积错误，影响最终推理准确性。需要一种方法让模型学会更简洁地推理。

Method: OPSDC（On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Compression）基于自我蒸馏思想：让同一模型在"简洁推理"指令下生成教师logits，然后在学生自身推理过程中最小化每个token的反向KL散度。无需真实答案、token预算或难度估计器，仅通过自我蒸馏实现压缩。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-14B模型上，MATH-500数据集上实现57-59%的token压缩，同时准确率提升9-16个百分点；在AIME 2024数据集上，14B模型准确率提升10个百分点，压缩率达到41%。

Conclusion: 推理模型产生的许多内容不仅是冗余的，而且是有害的，会随着每个不必要的token累积错误。OPSDC通过自我蒸馏自动实现智能压缩：对简单问题进行激进压缩，同时保留困难问题所需的深度思考，显著提升效率和准确性。

Abstract: Reasoning models think out loud, but much of what they say is noise. We introduce OPSDC (On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Compression), a method that teaches models to reason more concisely by
  distilling their own concise behavior back into themselves. The entire approach reduces to one idea: condition the same model on a "be concise" instruction to obtain teacher logits, and minimize per-token
  reverse KL on the student's own rollouts. No ground-truth answers, no token budgets, no difficulty estimators. Just self-distillation. Yet this simplicity belies surprising sophistication: OPSDC automatically
  compresses easy problems aggressively while preserving the deliberation needed for hard ones. On Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B, we achieve 57-59% token reduction on MATH-500 while improving accuracy by 9-16 points
  absolute. On AIME 2024, the 14B model gains 10 points with 41% compression. The secret? Much of what reasoning models produce is not just redundant-it is actively harmful, compounding errors with every
  unnecessary token.

</details>


### [99] [Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05440)
*Siqi Yang,Kai Yan,Alexander G. Schwing,Yu-Xiong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LWAIL是一种新颖的对抗性模仿学习框架，通过状态分布匹配在动态感知的潜在空间中计算Wasserstein距离，仅需少量状态专家轨迹即可达到专家级性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模仿学习方法需要大量中高质量演示数据和专家动作，但这些数据通常难以获取。需要开发能够在仅使用少量状态专家轨迹的情况下实现专家级性能的方法。

Method: 提出Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning (LWAIL)框架，采用状态分布匹配策略。通过预训练阶段学习Intention Conditioned Value Function (ICVF)，在动态感知的潜在空间中计算Wasserstein距离，仅需少量随机生成的状态数据即可捕捉状态空间的动态结构。

Result: 在多个MuJoCo环境中，LWAIL方法超越了先前基于Wasserstein的模仿学习方法和其他对抗性模仿学习方法，在各种任务上取得了更好的性能，仅需一个或几个状态专家轨迹即可达到专家级表现。

Conclusion: LWAIL通过动态感知潜在空间中的Wasserstein距离计算，显著减少了模仿学习对专家演示数据的依赖，仅需少量状态轨迹即可实现高效学习，为数据稀缺场景下的模仿学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Imitation Learning (IL) enables agents to mimic expert behavior by learning from demonstrations. However, traditional IL methods require large amounts of medium-to-high-quality demonstrations as well as actions of expert demonstrations, both of which are often unavailable. To reduce this need, we propose Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning (LWAIL), a novel adversarial imitation learning framework that focuses on state-only distribution matching. It benefits from the Wasserstein distance computed in a dynamics-aware latent space. This dynamics-aware latent space differs from prior work and is obtained via a pre-training stage, where we train the Intention Conditioned Value Function (ICVF) to capture a dynamics-aware structure of the state space using a small set of randomly generated state-only data. We show that this enhances the policy's understanding of state transitions, enabling the learning process to use only one or a few state-only expert episodes to achieve expert-level performance. Through experiments on multiple MuJoCo environments, we demonstrate that our method outperforms prior Wasserstein-based IL methods and prior adversarial IL methods, achieving better results across various tasks.

</details>


### [100] [SurvHTE-Bench: A Benchmark for Heterogeneous Treatment Effect Estimation in Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05483)
*Shahriar Noroozizadeh,Xiaobin Shen,Jeremy C. Weiss,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurvHTE-Bench是首个针对右删失生存数据的异质性处理效应估计的综合性基准测试，包含合成、半合成和真实数据集，用于系统评估现有方法在不同因果假设和生存动态下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析中的异质性处理效应估计在精准医学和个性化政策制定等高风险应用中至关重要，但由于删失、未观察到的反事实和复杂的识别假设，面临独特挑战。现有评估方法分散且不一致，缺乏统一标准。

Method: 构建了包含三个模块的基准测试：1）具有已知真实值的模块化合成数据集，系统变化因果假设和生存动态；2）结合真实世界协变量与模拟处理和结果的半合成数据集；3）来自双胞胎研究（已知真实值）和HIV临床试验的真实数据集。

Result: 在合成、半合成和真实世界设置中，首次对生存HTE方法在不同条件和现实假设违反下进行了严格比较，为因果生存方法的公平、可重复和可扩展评估奠定了基础。

Conclusion: SurvHTE-Bench为右删失生存数据的异质性处理效应估计建立了首个综合性基准测试框架，提供了数据、代码和评估标准，促进了该领域方法的公平比较和未来发展。

Abstract: Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects (HTEs) from right-censored survival data is critical in high-stakes applications such as precision medicine and individualized policy-making. Yet, the survival analysis setting poses unique challenges for HTE estimation due to censoring, unobserved counterfactuals, and complex identification assumptions. Despite recent advances, from Causal Survival Forests to survival meta-learners and outcome imputation approaches, evaluation practices remain fragmented and inconsistent. We introduce SurvHTE-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark for HTE estimation with censored outcomes. The benchmark spans (i) a modular suite of synthetic datasets with known ground truth, systematically varying causal assumptions and survival dynamics, (ii) semi-synthetic datasets that pair real-world covariates with simulated treatments and outcomes, and (iii) real-world datasets from a twin study (with known ground truth) and from an HIV clinical trial. Across synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real-world settings, we provide the first rigorous comparison of survival HTE methods under diverse conditions and realistic assumption violations. SurvHTE-Bench establishes a foundation for fair, reproducible, and extensible evaluation of causal survival methods. The data and code of our benchmark are available at: https://github.com/Shahriarnz14/SurvHTE-Bench .

</details>


### [101] [POET-X: Memory-efficient LLM Training by Scaling Orthogonal Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05500)
*Zeju Qiu,Lixin Liu,Adrian Weller,Han Shi,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POET-X：一种可扩展且内存高效的谱保持训练框架，通过正交等价变换优化权重矩阵，显著降低计算开销和内存消耗，使单张H100 GPU能够训练十亿参数LLM


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决POET框架在训练大型语言模型时存在的高内存消耗和计算开销问题，同时保持其训练稳定性和泛化优势

Method: 提出POET-X，一种可扩展的内存高效变体，通过改进的正交等价变换显著降低计算成本，保持谱保持特性但减少密集矩阵乘法操作

Result: POET-X在保持POET泛化和稳定性优势的同时，大幅提升吞吐量和内存效率，能够在单张Nvidia H100 GPU上预训练十亿参数LLM，而标准优化器如AdamW在相同设置下会内存不足

Conclusion: POET-X成功解决了原始POET框架的可扩展性问题，为高效稳定训练大规模语言模型提供了实用的解决方案，在计算效率和内存使用方面取得显著改进

Abstract: Efficient and stable training of large language models (LLMs) remains a core challenge in modern machine learning systems. To address this challenge, Reparameterized Orthogonal Equivalence Training (POET), a spectrum-preserving framework that optimizes each weight matrix through orthogonal equivalence transformation, has been proposed. Although POET provides strong training stability, its original implementation incurs high memory consumption and computational overhead due to intensive matrix multiplications. To overcome these limitations, we introduce POET-X, a scalable and memory-efficient variant that performs orthogonal equivalence transformations with significantly reduced computational cost. POET-X maintains the generalization and stability benefits of POET while achieving substantial improvements in throughput and memory efficiency. In our experiments, POET-X enables the pretraining of billion-parameter LLMs on a single Nvidia H100 GPU, and in contrast, standard optimizers such as AdamW run out of memory under the same settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Dynamic Model Routing and Cascading for Efficient LLM Inference: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04445)
*Yasmin Moslem,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了多LLM路由和级联方法，提出了一个三维概念框架，指出有效的路由系统需要在竞争目标间平衡，能超越单个最强模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在能力、成本和领域上的多样化增长，产生了在推理时智能选择模型的迫切需求。静态模型部署无法适应查询的复杂性和领域特性，导致性能次优和成本增加。动态路由系统能根据查询特征自适应选择模型，成为解决这一挑战的方案。

Method: 对最先进的多LLM路由和级联方法进行系统分析，涵盖多种路由范式：查询难度、人类偏好、聚类、不确定性量化、强化学习、多模态和级联。为每个范式分析代表性方法并检查关键权衡。引入一个三维概念框架来表征路由系统：决策时机、使用信息类型、计算方式。

Result: 分析表明，有效的多LLM路由需要在竞争目标间平衡。选择最优路由策略取决于部署和计算约束。设计良好的路由系统通过战略性地利用跨模型的专业能力，同时最大化效率增益，能够超越甚至最强大的单个模型。

Conclusion: 多LLM路由系统是解决模型多样化挑战的有效方案，但需要在竞争目标间平衡。实际系统通常是组合式的，集成多种范式以适应操作约束。开放挑战包括开发能跨不同架构、模态和应用泛化的路由机制。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large language models (LLMs) with diverse capabilities, costs, and domains has created a critical need for intelligent model selection at inference time. While smaller models suffice for routine queries, complex tasks demand more capable models. However, static model deployment does not account for the complexity and domain of incoming queries, leading to suboptimal performance and increased costs. Dynamic routing systems that adaptively select models based on query characteristics have emerged as a solution to this challenge.
  We provide a systematic analysis of state-of-the-art multi-LLM routing and cascading approaches. In contrast to mixture-of-experts architectures, which route within a single model, we study routing across multiple independently trained LLMs. We cover diverse routing paradigms, including query difficulty, human preferences, clustering, uncertainty quantification, reinforcement learning, multimodality, and cascading. For each paradigm, we analyze representative methods and examine key trade-offs. Beyond taxonomy, we introduce a conceptual framework that characterizes routing systems along three dimensions: when decisions are made, what information is used, and how they are computed. This perspective highlights that practical systems are often compositional, integrating multiple paradigms under operational constraints.
  Our analysis demonstrates that effective multi-LLM routing requires balancing competing objectives. Choosing the optimal routing strategy depends on deployment and computational constraints. Well-designed routing systems can outperform even the most powerful individual models by strategically leveraging specialized capabilities across models while maximizing efficiency gains. Meanwhile, open challenges remain in developing routing mechanisms that generalize across diverse architectures, modalities, and applications.

</details>


### [103] [Selfish Cooperation Towards Low-Altitude Economy: Integrated Multi-Service Deployment with Resilient Federated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04779)
*Yuxuan Yang,Bin Lyu,Abbas Jamalipour*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于真实性保证拍卖机制和弹性联邦强化学习的解决方案，用于优化低空经济中多服务提供商竞争环境下的通信与计算资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空经济作为6G时代的新兴范式，吸引了大量服务提供商参与，导致服务部署竞争加剧。需要解决多服务提供商动态部署无人机、优化通信计算资源分配的问题，同时应对传输错误和恶意竞争等挑战。

Method: 设计了真实性保证的拍卖机制来解决服务提供商之间的部署竞争，采用博弈论分析证明资源分配问题的可解性。开发了弹性联邦强化学习解决方案，具有强容错能力，能够有效应对传输错误和恶意竞争，同时促进自利服务提供商之间的潜在合作。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与基准方法相比，所提出的方法显著提高了服务性能和鲁棒性，为竞争性低空经济服务部署提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Conclusion: 该研究为低空经济中多服务提供商的竞争性服务部署问题提供了有效的解决方案，通过拍卖机制和联邦强化学习的结合，实现了资源优化分配和系统鲁棒性的提升。

Abstract: The low-altitude economy (LAE) is a rapidly emerging paradigm that builds a service-centric economic ecosystem through large-scale and sustainable uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV)-enabled service provisioning, reflecting the transition of the 6G era from technological advancement toward commercial deployment. The significant market potential of LAE attracts an increasing number of service providers (SPs), resulting in intensified competition in service deployment. In this paper, we study a realistic LAE scenario in which multiple SPs dynamically deploy UAVs to deliver multiple services to user hotspots, aiming to jointly optimize communication and computation resource allocation. To resolve deployment competition among SPs, an authenticity-guaranteed auction mechanism is designed, and game-theoretic analysis is conducted to establish the solvability of the proposed resource allocation problem. Furthermore, a resilient federated reinforcement learning (FRL)-based solution is developed with strong fault tolerance, effectively countering transmission errors and malicious competition while facilitating potential cooperation among self-interested SPs. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly improves service performance and robustness compared with baseline methods, providing a practical and scalable solution for competitive LAE service deployment.

</details>


### [104] [Adaptive Personalized Federated Reinforcement Learning for RIS-Assisted Aerial Relays in SAGINs with Fluid Antennas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04788)
*Yuxuan Yang,Bin Lyu,Abbas Jamalipour*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了RIS辅助无人机中继的SAGIN系统，通过个性化联邦强化学习优化无人机轨迹和RIS相位控制，以最大化多热点下行速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAGIN中跨层设施和无线电增强技术的紧密集成导致显著的环境动态性和异构性，给大规模SAGIN的系统建模和优化带来根本性挑战。

Method: 提出自适应个性化联邦强化学习算法，通过分层Stackelberg博弈分析可解性，优化无人机轨迹和RIS相位控制。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法在动态异构SAGIN场景中具有优越性能，验证了个性化策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应个性化FRL算法能够有效应对SAGIN中的动态异构环境，为大规模SAGIN优化提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Space-air-ground integrated networks (SAGINs) interconnect satellites, uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs), and ground devices to enable flexible and ubiquitous wireless services. The integration of reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) and fluid antenna systems (FASs) further enhances radio environment controllability. However, the tight integration of cross-layer facilities and radio enhancement technologies leads to pronounced environmental dynamics and heterogeneity, posing fundamental challenges for system modeling and optimization in large-scale SAGINs. This paper investigates a SAGIN in which low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations communicate with multiple ground hotspots via RIS-assisted UAV relays, serving both FAS-equipped and conventional users. A system model is developed that explicitly captures satellite mobility, UAV trajectories, RIS phase control, and heterogeneous user reception capabilities. Accordingly, a multi-hotspot downlink rate maximization problem is studied, whose solvability is analyzed through a hierarchical Stackelberg game. To address heterogeneous and time-varying multi-hotspot environments, an adaptive personalized federated reinforcement learning (FRL) algorithm is proposed for adaptive optimization of UAV trajectories and RIS phase controls. Simulation results demonstrate superior performance and validate the effectiveness of personalization in dynamic heterogeneous SAGIN scenarios.

</details>


### [105] [Analysis of Proactive Uncoordinated Techniques to Mitigate Interference in FMCW Automotive Radars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04944)
*Alessandro Bazzi,Francesco Miccoli,Fabrizio Cuccoli,Luca Facheris,Vincent Martinez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了FMCW雷达在密集交通场景中的相互干扰问题，分析了三种无需车辆间协调的主动缓解方法，发现啁啾级频率跳变结合足够带宽是最有效的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ADAS系统中雷达数量的增加，同一频谱内的雷达相互干扰问题日益严重，可能导致系统故障和安全风险。需要评估干扰影响并寻找有效的缓解方法。

Method: 研究假设所有车辆都配备FMCW雷达，考虑直接和反射信号的干扰影响，评估三种主动缓解方法：(1)帧级随机载波频率跳变，(2)啁啾级随机载波频率跳变，(3)基于罗盘的方向性方法（可与前两种方法结合）。通过真实交通场景模拟和考虑相关干扰的新模型进行分析。

Result: 密集场景下雷达故障风险很高。啁啾级频率跳变在结合足够大带宽时是最有效的干扰缓解方法，能确保系统可靠性。基于罗盘的方法效果有限，不值得增加系统复杂度。

Conclusion: 在密集交通环境中，啁啾级频率跳变配合足够带宽是缓解FMCW雷达相互干扰的最有效策略，而基于罗盘的方法由于效果有限且增加复杂度，不建议采用。

Abstract: Modern vehicles increasingly rely on advanced driver-assistance systems (ADAS), with radars playing a key role due to their cost-effectiveness and reliable performance. However, the growing number of radars operating in the same spectrum raises concerns about mutual interference, which could lead to system malfunctions and potential safety risks. This study focuses on a scenario in which all vehicles are equipped with frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars, and it assesses the impact of interference on radar functionality - expressed in terms of probability of failure - by considering both direct and reflected signals. The radars may employ one of the following proactive mitigation methods to reduce the impact of interference, all of which require no inter-vehicle coordination but differ in complexity: (i) random carrier-frequency hopping on a frame-by-frame basis, (ii) random carrier-frequency hopping on a chirp-by-chirp basis, and (iii) a directional, compass-based method specifically addressing interference from opposite directions, which can be combined with either of the two previous methods. In this work, we assume realistic simulated road traffic scenarios and develop a novel model that captures correlated interference and accounts for the main radar setting parameters. Results reveal that dense scenarios pose a high risk of radar malfunctions. Among the analyzed methods, chirp-by-chirp frequency hopping emerges as the most effective approach to mitigate interference and ensure system reliability, but only when combined with a sufficiently large bandwidth. The compass-based method, on the other hand, shows limited effectiveness and appears not worth the additional system complexity.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [How Effective Are Publicly Accessible Deepfake Detection Tools? A Comparative Evaluation of Open-Source and Free-to-Use Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04456)
*Michael Rettinger,Ben Beaumont,Nhien-An Le-Khac,Hong-Hanh Nguyen-Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次对六种公开可用的深度伪造检测工具进行跨范式评估，涵盖法证分析工具和AI分类器，发现法证工具召回率高但特异性差，AI分类器则相反，人类评估者显著优于所有自动化工具


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造图像泛滥给数字媒体真实性验证带来挑战，虽然检测算法研究丰富，但对实际使用的公开工具进行实证评估的研究仍然稀缺

Method: 采用交叉范式评估六种工具：法证分析工具（InVID & WeVerify, FotoForensics, Forensically）和AI分类器（DecopyAI, FaceOnLive, Bitmind）。由具有执法经验的专业调查人员使用盲法协议，在包含真实、篡改和AI生成图像的数据集（DF40, CelebDF, CASIA-v2）上进行评估

Result: 1. 法证工具召回率高但特异性差，AI分类器则呈现相反模式；2. 人类评估者显著优于所有自动化工具；3. 人机分歧不对称，在绝大多数不一致案例中人类判断占主导地位

Conclusion: 讨论了从业者工作流程的影响，并指出了当前检测能力的关键差距，强调人类专家在深度伪造检测中的持续重要性

Abstract: The proliferation of deepfake imagery poses escalating challenges for practitioners tasked with verifying digital media authenticity. While detection algorithm research is abundant, empirical evaluations of publicly accessible tools that practitioners actually use remain scarce. This paper presents the first cross-paradigm evaluation of six tools, spanning two complementary detection approaches: forensic analysis tools (InVID \& WeVerify, FotoForensics, Forensically) and AI-based classifiers (DecopyAI, FaceOnLive, Bitmind). Both tool categories were evaluated by professional investigators with law enforcement experience using blinded protocols across datasets comprising authentic, tampered, and AI-generated images sourced from DF40, CelebDF, and CASIA-v2. We report three principal findings: forensic tools exhibit high recall but poor specificity, while AI classifiers demonstrate the inverse pattern; human evaluators substantially outperform all automated tools; and human-AI disagreement is asymmetric, with human judgment prevailing in the vast majority of discordant cases. We discuss implications for practitioner workflows and identify critical gaps in current detection capabilities.

</details>


### [107] [Benchmark of Benchmarks: Unpacking Influence and Code Repository Quality in LLM Safety Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04459)
*Junjie Chu,Xinyue Shen,Ye Leng,Michael Backes,Yun Shen,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次对LLM安全基准测试进行多维度评估，分析31个基准测试和382个非基准测试论文，发现基准测试在学术影响力上无显著优势，且作者知名度与代码质量存在错位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM安全研究快速增长，基准测试对追踪趋势和系统比较至关重要，但尚不清楚某些基准测试为何获得关注，也缺乏对其学术影响力和代码质量的系统评估

Method: 对LLM安全基准测试进行首次多维度评估，基于五个指标评估影响力，结合自动化和人工评估分析代码质量，涵盖提示注入、越狱和幻觉三个领域，分析31个基准测试和382个非基准测试论文

Result: 基准测试论文在学术影响力（如引用次数和密度）上无显著优势；作者知名度与论文影响力相关，但两者均与代码质量无显著相关性；代码和补充材料有较大改进空间：仅39%仓库可直接使用，16%包含无缺陷安装指南，仅6%涉及伦理考虑

Conclusion: LLM安全基准测试的学术影响力与代码质量存在错位，需要建立更高标准，特别是知名研究者应带头提升代码质量和伦理考量

Abstract: The rapid growth of research in LLM safety makes it hard to track all advances. Benchmarks are therefore crucial for capturing key trends and enabling systematic comparisons. Yet, it remains unclear why certain benchmarks gain prominence, and no systematic assessment has been conducted on their academic influence or code quality. This paper fills this gap by presenting the first multi-dimensional evaluation of the influence (based on five metrics) and code quality (based on both automated and human assessment) on LLM safety benchmarks, analyzing 31 benchmarks and 382 non-benchmarks across prompt injection, jailbreak, and hallucination. We find that benchmark papers show no significant advantage in academic influence (e.g., citation count and density) over non-benchmark papers. We uncover a key misalignment: while author prominence correlates with paper influence, neither author prominence nor paper influence shows a significant correlation with code quality. Our results also indicate substantial room for improvement in code and supplementary materials: only 39% of repositories are ready-to-use, 16% include flawless installation guides, and a mere 6% address ethical considerations. Given that the work of prominent researchers tends to attract greater attention, they need to lead the effort in setting higher standards.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Input Guardrails: Reconstructing Cross-Agent Semantic Flows for Execution-Aware Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04469)
*Yangyang Wei,Yijie Xu,Zhenyuan Li,Xiangmin Shen,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个名为SysName的框架，用于检测多智能体系统中的安全风险，通过分析跨智能体语义流来识别攻击向量，而非传统的静态输入过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统成为复杂任务编排的事实标准，但其依赖自主执行和非结构化智能体间通信引入了严重风险（如间接提示注入），这些风险容易绕过传统的输入防护机制。

Method: 提出SysName框架，将防御范式从静态输入过滤转向执行感知分析。通过提取和重构跨智能体语义流，将碎片化的操作原语合成为连续的行为轨迹，从而获得系统活动的整体视图。利用监督LLM来审查这些轨迹，识别数据流违规、控制流偏差和意图不一致等异常。

Result: 经验评估表明，SysName能有效检测超过10种不同的复合攻击向量，在节点级和路径级端到端攻击检测中分别达到85.3%和66.7%的F1分数。

Conclusion: SysName框架通过执行感知分析和跨智能体语义流重构，为多智能体系统提供了一种有效的安全检测方法，能够识别传统防护机制难以发现的复杂攻击。

Abstract: Multi-Agent System is emerging as the \textit{de facto} standard for complex task orchestration. However, its reliance on autonomous execution and unstructured inter-agent communication introduces severe risks, such as indirect prompt injection, that easily circumvent conventional input guardrails. To address this, we propose \SysName, a framework that shifts the defensive paradigm from static input filtering to execution-aware analysis. By extracting and reconstructing Cross-Agent Semantic Flows, \SysName synthesizes fragmented operational primitives into contiguous behavioral trajectories, enabling a holistic view of system activity. We leverage a Supervisor LLM to scrutinize these trajectories, identifying anomalies across data flow violations, control flow deviations, and intent inconsistencies. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that \SysName effectively detects over ten distinct compound attack vectors, achieving F1-scores of 85.3\% and 66.7\% for node-level and path-level end-to-end attack detection, respectively. The source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MAScope-71DC.

</details>


### [109] [ShieldBypass: On the Persistence of Impedance Leakage Beyond EM Shielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04801)
*Md Sadik Awal,Md Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 主动射频探测可检测屏蔽系统中执行相关的阻抗变化，即使辐射发射被抑制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁屏蔽虽能抑制辐射发射和被动电磁侧信道泄漏，但无法防御主动探测攻击。本研究探讨在屏蔽抑制辐射发射的情况下，阻抗调制的反向散射是否仍然存在。

Method: 通过注入受控射频信号并分析反射信号，评估在三种行业标准屏蔽下，基于FPGA和微控制器原型的处理器工作负载特征。比较被动电磁测量和反向散射响应的区分能力。

Result: 在屏蔽条件下，被动电磁测量失去区分能力，而反向散射响应仍保持可分离性。状态相关的阻抗变化在屏蔽主要衰减频带之外的频率上仍然可观测。

Conclusion: 主动射频探测能够暴露屏蔽系统中执行相关的行为，表明需要在硬件安全评估流程中考虑基于主动阻抗的探测攻击。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) shielding is widely used to suppress radiated emissions and limit passive EM side-channel leakage. However, shielding does not address active probing, where an adversary injects external radio-frequency (RF) signals and observes the device's reflective response. This work studies whether such impedance-modulated backscattering persists when radiated emissions are suppressed by shielding. By injecting controlled RF signals and analyzing the reflections, we demonstrate that state-dependent impedance variations remain observable at frequencies outside the shields' primary attenuation band. Using processors implemented on FPGA and microcontroller prototypes, and evaluating workload profiles under three industry-standard shields, we find that passive EM measurements lose discriminative power under shielding, while backscattering responses remain separable. These results indicate that active RF probing can expose execution-dependent behavior even in shielded systems, motivating the need to consider active impedance-based probing within hardware security evaluation flows.

</details>


### [110] [When Denoising Becomes Unsigning: Theoretical and Empirical Analysis of Watermark Fragility Under Diffusion-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04696)
*Fai Gu,Qiyu Tang,Te Wen,Emily Davis,Finn Carter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 扩散编辑会破坏传统鲁棒水印：扩散编辑过程注入高斯噪声并去噪，将水印作为高频信号衰减，导致解码失败


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究扩散编辑与传统鲁棒水印之间的冲突：扩散编辑可能无意中破坏为抵抗传统失真而设计的水印机制

Method: 建立扩散编辑的统一框架：在潜在空间注入高斯噪声，通过去噪过程投影回自然图像流形；使用信息论工具形式化水印退化过程

Result: 证明对于广泛的像素级水印编码器/解码器，水印载荷与编辑输出之间的互信息随编辑强度增加而衰减至零，解码错误接近随机猜测

Conclusion: 扩散编辑会系统性地破坏传统鲁棒水印，需要新的水印方案设计指南以适应生成式变换时代

Abstract: Robust invisible watermarking systems aim to embed imperceptible payloads that remain decodable after common post-processing such as JPEG compression, cropping, and additive noise. In parallel, diffusion-based image editing has rapidly matured into a default transformation layer for modern content pipelines, enabling instruction-based editing, object insertion and composition, and interactive geometric manipulation. This paper studies a subtle but increasingly consequential interaction between these trends: diffusion-based editing procedures may unintentionally compromise, and in extreme cases practically bypass, robust watermarking mechanisms that were explicitly engineered to survive conventional distortions. We develop a unified view of diffusion editors that (i) inject substantial Gaussian noise in a latent space and (ii) project back to the natural image manifold via learned denoising dynamics. Under this view, watermark payloads behave as low-energy, high-frequency signals that are systematically attenuated by the forward diffusion step and then treated as nuisance variation by the reverse generative process. We formalize this degradation using information-theoretic tools, proving that for broad classes of pixel-level watermark encoders/decoders the mutual information between the watermark payload and the edited output decays toward zero as the editing strength increases, yielding decoding error close to random guessing. We complement the theory with a realistic hypothetical experimental protocol and tables spanning representative watermarking methods and representative diffusion editors. Finally, we discuss ethical implications, responsible disclosure norms, and concrete design guidelines for watermarking schemes that remain meaningful in the era of generative transformations.

</details>


### [111] [Efficient Privacy-Preserving Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication Using Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04742)
*Yang Gao,Gang Quan,Wujie Wen,Scott Piersall,Qian Lou,Liqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首个将同态加密与稀疏矩阵向量乘法高效集成的框架，提出CSSC压缩格式优化加密稀疏矩阵计算


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏矩阵向量乘法是科学计算、数据分析和机器学习的基础操作，处理敏感数据时需要保护隐私。同态加密虽能实现隐私保护计算，但在稀疏矩阵向量乘法中的应用尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出名为压缩稀疏排序列（CSSC）的新型压缩矩阵格式，专门设计用于优化加密稀疏矩阵计算。该格式通过保持稀疏性和实现高效的密文打包，显著减少存储和计算开销。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法在处理时间和内存使用方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了隐私保护的稀疏矩阵向量乘法，为联邦学习、加密数据库、科学计算等安全应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV) is a fundamental operation in scientific computing, data analysis, and machine learning. When the data being processed are sensitive, preserving privacy becomes critical, and homomorphic encryption (HE) has emerged as a leading approach for addressing this challenge. Although HE enables privacy-preserving computation, its application to SpMV has remained largely unaddressed. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first framework that efficiently integrates HE with SpMV, addressing the dual challenges of computational efficiency and data privacy. In particular, we introduce a novel compressed matrix format, named Compressed Sparse Sorted Column (CSSC), which is specifically designed to optimize encrypted sparse matrix computations. By preserving sparsity and enabling efficient ciphertext packing, CSSC significantly reduces storage and computational overhead. Our experimental results on real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves significant gains in both processing time and memory usage. This study advances privacy-preserving SpMV and lays the groundwork for secure applications in federated learning, encrypted databases, scientific computing, and beyond.

</details>


### [112] [Osmosis Distillation: Model Hijacking with the Fewest Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04859)
*Yuchen Shi,Huajie Chen,Heng Xu,Zhiquan Liu,Jialiang Shen,Chi Liu,Shuai Zhou,Tianqing Zhu,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为"渗透蒸馏攻击"的新型模型劫持攻击方法，揭示了在迁移学习中使用第三方合成数据集的安全威胁


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习和数据集蒸馏的结合虽然能提高性能，但存在未发现的安全威胁。攻击者只需在合成数据集中添加少量中毒样本，就能实施模型劫持攻击

Method: 提出了渗透蒸馏攻击，这是一种针对深度学习模型的新型模型劫持策略，使用最少的样本实现攻击。该方法通过在合成数据集中植入少量中毒样本，使模型在保持原始任务性能的同时执行隐藏的恶意任务

Result: 在各种数据集上的综合评估表明，渗透蒸馏攻击在隐藏任务中实现了高攻击成功率，同时在原始任务中保持了高模型效用。蒸馏后的渗透集还能在不同模型架构间实现模型劫持，在迁移学习中具有显著的攻击性能和模型效用

Conclusion: 必须提高对在迁移学习中使用第三方合成数据集的安全意识，因为即使少量中毒样本也能导致严重的模型劫持攻击

Abstract: Transfer learning is devised to leverage knowledge from pre-trained models to solve new tasks with limited data and computational resources. Meanwhile, dataset distillation has emerged to synthesize a compact dataset that preserves critical information from the original large dataset. Therefore, a combination of transfer learning and dataset distillation offers promising performance in evaluations. However, a non-negligible security threat remains undiscovered in transfer learning using synthetic datasets generated by dataset distillation methods, where an adversary can perform a model hijacking attack with only a few poisoned samples in the synthetic dataset. To reveal this threat, we propose Osmosis Distillation (OD) attack, a novel model hijacking strategy that targets deep learning models using the fewest samples. Comprehensive evaluations on various datasets demonstrate that the OD attack attains high attack success rates in hidden tasks while preserving high model utility in original tasks. Furthermore, the distilled osmosis set enables model hijacking across diverse model architectures, allowing model hijacking in transfer learning with considerable attack performance and model utility. We argue that awareness of using third-party synthetic datasets in transfer learning must be raised.

</details>


### [113] [Modification to Fully Homomorphic Modified Rivest Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04952)
*Sona Alex,Bian Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FHMRS存在安全漏洞，mFHMRS通过修改方案来缓解该漏洞


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现完全同态修改Rivest方案(FHMRS)存在安全漏洞，需要提出改进方案来增强安全性

Method: 分析FHMRS的安全漏洞，并提出修改后的mFHMRS方案来缓解该漏洞

Result: 识别了FHMRS的安全问题，设计了mFHMRS作为改进方案

Conclusion: mFHMRS通过修改原方案有效缓解了FHMRS的安全漏洞，提升了方案的安全性

Abstract: This document details the Fully Homomorphic Modified Rivest Scheme (FHMRS), a security issue in FHMRS, and a modification to FHMRS (mFHMRS) to mitigate the security issue.

</details>


### [114] [A Practical Post-Quantum Distributed Ledger Protocol for Financial Institutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05005)
*Yeoh Wei Zhu,Naresh Goud Boddu,Yao Ma,Shaltiel Eloul,Giulio Golinelli,Yash Satsangi,Rob Otter,Kaushik Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于格的后量子加密账本交易方案，解决金融机构隐私与审计需求，采用新型零知识证明技术实现公开可验证交易


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融机构存在效率问题，分布式账本技术可解决但面临隐私挑战。现有Ring-CT模型不适合金融机构采用，需要满足保密性和可审计性要求的方案

Method: 提出基于格的后量子交易方案，利用多种零知识证明技术，引入新型承诺等式验证方法（无法在重新承诺时打开原承诺），构建紧凑范围证明实现单/多资产高效公开可验证交易

Result: 构建了适用于金融机构的加密账本交易方案，提供安全性分析，所采用的技术和证明具有独立研究价值

Conclusion: 该方案解决了金融机构采用分布式账本时的隐私与审计矛盾，为后量子安全加密账本提供了可行技术路径

Abstract: Traditional financial institutions face inefficiencies that can be addressed by distributed ledger technology. However, a primary barrier to adoption is the privacy concerns surrounding publicly available transaction data. Existing private protocols for distributed ledger that focus on the Ring-CT model are not suitable for adoption for financial institutions. We propose a post-quantum, lattice-based transaction scheme for encrypted ledgers which better aligns with institutions' requirements for confidentiality and audit-ability. The construction leverages various zero-knowledge proof techniques, and introduces a new method for equating two commitment messages, without the capability to open one of the commitment during the re-commitment. Subsequently, we build a publicly verifiable transaction scheme that is efficient for single or multi-assets, by introducing a new compact range-proof. We then provide a security analysis of it. The techniques used and the proofs constructed could be of independent interest.

</details>


### [115] [Good-Enough LLM Obfuscation (GELO)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05035)
*Anatoly Belikov,Ilya Fedotov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GELO是一种轻量级隐私保护推理协议，通过每批次可逆混合隐藏隐藏状态，防止共享加速器上的KV缓存和隐藏状态被窃取，在保持输出精度的同时仅增加20-30%延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在共享加速器上部署时，攻击者可通过读取设备内存观察KV缓存和隐藏状态，威胁开源模型的提示隐私。现有密码学保护方案（如MPC和FHE）速度太慢，而静态混淆方案在多轮统计攻击下会失效。

Method: GELO采用每批次新鲜可逆混合方案：TEE为每个卸载的投影采样随机矩阵A，计算U=AH，将U和权重W卸载到加速器，返回时应用A⁻¹恢复正确输出。提出两种防御：(1)非正交混合掩盖Gram矩阵；(2)正交混合添加高能量"屏蔽"向量污染高阶统计量。

Result: 在Llama-2 7B上，GELO能保持float32输出精度，与低精度基线性能接近，主要矩阵乘法卸载仅增加20-30%延迟开销，并能抵御ICA/BSS和基于锚点的攻击。

Conclusion: GELO提供了一种实用的隐私保护推理方案，在共享加速器环境中平衡了隐私保护、计算效率和输出精度，解决了现有方案在速度或安全性方面的不足。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly served on shared accelerators where an adversary with read access to device memory can observe KV caches and hidden states, threatening prompt privacy for open-source models. Cryptographic protections such as MPC and FHE offer strong guarantees but remain one to two orders of magnitude too slow for interactive inference, while static obfuscation schemes break under multi-run statistical attacks once the model is known. We present GELO (Good-Enough LLM Obfuscation), a lightweight protocol for privacy-preserving inference that limits information leakage from untrusted accelerator observations by hiding hidden states with fresh, per-batch invertible mixing. For each offloaded projection, the TEE samples a random matrix A, forms $U = AH$, offloads U and weights W to the accelerator, and then applies $A^-1$ on return, so that $A^-1 ((AH)W ) = HW$ and outputs are unchanged. Because mixing is never reused across batches, the attacker faces only a single-batch blind source separation problem. We analyze information leakage and introduce two practical defenses: (i) non-orthogonal mixing to mask Gram matrices, and (ii) orthogonal mixing augmented with a small fraction of high-energy "shield" vectors that pollute higher-order statistics. On Llama-2 7B, GELO preserves float32 outputs exactly, closely matches low-precision baselines, offloads the dominant matrix multiplications with about 20-30% latency overhead, and defeats a range of ICA/BSS and anchor-based attacks.

</details>


### [116] [Robust Single-message Shuffle Differential Privacy Protocol for Accurate Distribution Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05073)
*Xiaoguang Li,Hanyi Wang,Yaowei Huang,Jungang Yang,Qingqing Ye,Haonan Yan,Ke Pan,Zhe Sun,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ASP协议用于基于洗牌器的差分隐私分布估计，在效用、消息复杂度和抗数据投毒攻击鲁棒性三个指标上优于现有方法


<details>
